# OSRS Path - full route export (app version 2.54.0)

Exported 2026-10-14 from the program's data files (steps.json, questStages.json, stages.json, goals.json). Every step is written out with everything the program shows for it: where to go, what to bring and where each item comes from, the quick options, the walkthrough, what the game plugin highlights and when it ticks the step off by itself, and, for quests, the stage table line by line (what the game shows for each value of the quest variable).

- Part 1 is the free-to-play route in order (51 steps, stages 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6). Stage 6 is the optional way to a Bond: a members account skips it.
- Part 2 is the members route (16 steps, stages 7, 8, 9), done after Dragon Slayer I.
- Part 3 is the target levels at the end of each stage.

## Stages

- Stage 1: Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests - S1-01, S1-02, S1-03, S1-04, S1-05, S1-06, S1-07, S1-08, S1-09, S1-10, S1-12, S1-13
- Stage 2: Varrock, teleport magic and key quests - S2-01, S2-02, S2-03, S2-04, S2-05, S2-06, S2-07, S2-08, S2-09, S2-10, S2-11, S2-12
- Stage 3: Access to the mines and gear preparation - S3-01, S3-02, S3-03, S3-04, S3-05, S3-06, S3-07, S3-08
- Stage 4: Preparing for Dragon Slayer I - S4-01, S4-02, S4-03, S4-04, S4-05
- Stage 5: Dragon Slayer I: the F2P climax - S5-01, S5-02, S5-03, S5-04, S5-05, S5-06, S5-07, S5-08, S5-09
- Stage 6: Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership) - S6-01, S6-02, S6-03, S6-04, S6-05
- Stage 7 (members): Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding - S7-01, S7-02, S7-03, S7-04, S7-05
- Stage 8 (members): Global transport and stamina - S8-01, S8-02, S8-03, S8-04, S8-05, S8-06
- Stage 9 (members): Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves - S9-01, S9-02, S9-03, S9-04, S9-05

## Part 1 - F2P route (51 steps)

## Stage 1 - Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests

### S1-01 - Set up the RuneLite client

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type prep
- Where: The RuneLite client, launched through the Jagex Launcher.
- How: Enable the Quest Helper, Ground Items, Item Prices, Idle Notifier and GPU (or 117 HD) plugins. Turn on two-factor authentication (2FA) on your Jagex Account right away: without it Stronghold of Security (step S1-09) will not hand out the 10,000 coins.
- Starting money: After Tutorial Island you have only about 25 coins. The first real money comes from Stronghold of Security (10,000) and cowhides (S1-13), so do not buy anything until then.
- Done when: The Quest Helper panel is visible in the right-hand tab of the client and two-factor authentication is on.

### S1-02 - Sort out your inventory at the Lumbridge bank

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type prep
- Where: The Lumbridge Castle bank, on the top floor of the castle.
- Floor: 2nd floor
- Map: Lumbridge Castle bank (top floor) (3208, 3219, plane 2)
- How: Keep only an axe, a pickaxe, a net and a tinderbox in your inventory; put everything else in the bank.
- Done when: Your inventory is free for quests and gathering.
- Items required:
  - Bronze axe x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, buy one from Bob's Brilliant Axes in south Lumbridge for 16 gp or pick one up near Farmer Fred's house. [from: Bob; item id 1351]
  - Bronze pickaxe x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, buy one from Bob's Brilliant Axes in Lumbridge for 1 gp. [from: Bob; item id 1265]
  - Small fishing net x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, there is one beside the Fishing tutor in Lumbridge Swamp (it respawns), or buy one from Gerrant in Port Sarim for 5 gp. [from: Fishing tutor; item id 303]
  - Tinderbox x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, buy one in the Lumbridge General Store (north-east of the castle) for 1 gp. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 590]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Bronze axe, Bronze pickaxe, Small fishing net, Tinderbox
  - No automatic completion: you mark this step yourself.

### S1-03 - Cook's Assistant

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: The cook's kitchen in Lumbridge Castle (3208, 3214, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Flour — the wheat field by Mill Lane Mill (3163, 3289, plane 0) [items: Pot of flour] - Pick the wheat, climb to the hopper on the 2nd floor of the mill, pull the lever and go down to the flour bin; you need an empty pot.
  - Milk — the dairy cow pasture north of the mill (3177, 3315, plane 0) [NPC Dairy cow] [items: Bucket of milk] - Dairy cow: the one with a bell; use an empty bucket on it.
  - Egg — the chicken coop north-west of Farmer Fred's house (3181, 3288, plane 0) [items: Egg]
- NPC: Cook - The kitchen in the south wing of Lumbridge Castle, Ground floor. Dialogue: 1 → 1 ("What's wrong?" → "Can I help?")
- Pro tip: Cook your early food on the castle kitchen's range (Cook's range): food burns far less often there!
- Reward: 1 QP, 300 Cooking XP, access to the castle range.
- Done when: The quest is green in the quest list and a second pot of flour is in the bank (for S2-10).
- Items required:
  - Pot of flour x1 - Pick wheat on the field north of the castle, put it into the hopper on the 2nd floor of Lumbridge Windmill, pull the lever and collect the flour into a pot on the ground floor. [obtained during the step; item id 1933]
  - Bucket of milk x1 - Buy a bucket from the General Store for 2 gp and milk a dairy cow (the one with a bell) in the pasture north of the mill. [obtained during the step; item id 1927]
  - Egg x1 - Pick one up in the chicken coop north-west of Farmer Fred's house (north of the castle). [obtained during the step; item id 1944]
- Items recommended:
  - Pot of flour x1 - Make 1 more pot of flour at the mill: it is needed for the paste in Prince Ali Rescue (S2-10). Pots lie in the castle kitchen and are sold in the General Store for 1 gp. [item id 1933]
- Keep for S2-10: Pot of flour x1 (bank) - Prince Ali Rescue needs a pot of flour for the paste.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: The cook's kitchen in Lumbridge Castle (3208, 3214, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Cook, Dairy cow
  - Objects highlighted: Cooking range, Wheat, Hopper, Hopper controls, Flour bin
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Pot of flour, Bucket of milk, Egg, Pot, Bucket, Grain
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “What's wrong?” → “Can I help?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Cook's Assistant and holding 1 x Pot of flour
- Walkthrough:
  1. Buy a bucket from the Lumbridge General Store.
  2. Buy a pot from the Lumbridge General Store.
  3. Pick up an egg from the farm north of Lumbridge.
  4. Pick some wheat in the field north of Lumbridge.
  5. Climb the ladder in the mill north of Lumbridge to the top floor.
  6. Fill the hopper with the wheat.
  7. Operate the hopper controls.
  8. Climb down the ladder to the ground floor.
  9. Collect the flour from the flour bin into your pot.
  10. Milk the dairy cow north of Lumbridge. You need a bucket.
  11. Give the Cook in Lumbridge Castle's kitchen the three items to finish the quest. Dialogue: “What's wrong?” → “Can I help?” → “Yes”.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):

      1. Buy a bucket from the Lumbridge General Store.
      2. Buy a pot from the Lumbridge General Store.
  - Egg

      3. Fill the hopper with the wheat.

      5. Climb down the ladder to the ground floor.
      6. Collect the flour from the flour bin into your pot.
  - Milk
      1. Milk the dairy cow north of Lumbridge. You need a bucket.
  - Hand in the items
      1. Give the Cook in Lumbridge Castle's kitchen the three items to finish the quest. Dialogue: “What's wrong?” → “Can I help?” → “Yes”.

### S1-04 - Sheep Shearer

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Farmer Fred's house (3189, 3272, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Spinning wheel — Lumbridge Castle, 1st floor (3209, 3213, plane 1) [items: Ball of wool] - Shear the wool from the sheep in the pen by Fred's house, then spin it here.
- NPC: Fred the Farmer - A house north-west of Lumbridge, just past the mill, Ground floor. Dialogue: 1 → 1 ("I'm looking for a quest." → "Yes.")
- Pro tip: Do not give Fred more than 20 balls! Put exactly 3 in the bank: it saves a lot of time in the Prince Ali Rescue quest.
- Reward: 1 QP, 150 XP Crafting, 60 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green and 3 balls of wool are in the bank.
- Items required:
  - Shears x1 - Take one for free from the table in Fred's house or buy one in the General Store for 1 gp. [from: Fred the Farmer; obtained during the step; item id 1735]
  - Ball of wool x23 (20 to hand in, 3 for the bank) - Shear 23 wool from the sheep in the pen and spin it on the spinning wheel on the 1st floor of Lumbridge Castle. [obtained during the step; item id 1759]
- Keep for S2-10: Ball of wool x3 (bank) - Prince Ali Rescue needs 3 balls of wool for the wig.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: Farmer Fred's house (3189, 3272, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Fred the Farmer, Sheep
  - Objects highlighted: Spinning wheel
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Shears, Wool, Ball of wool
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm looking for a quest.” → “Yes.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Sheep Shearer and holding 3 x Ball of wool
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Fred the Farmer north of Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest” → “Yes, okay. I can do that” → “Yes”.
  2. Pick up the shears in Fred's house.
  3. Shear 20 wool from the sheep in the nearby field.
  4. Climb the staircase in Lumbridge Castle: the spinning wheel is on the first floor.
  5. Spin the wool into balls of wool on the spinning wheel.
  6. Climb back down the staircase. Dialogue: “Climb down the stairs”.
  7. Give Fred the balls of wool (unnoted). If you only have some of them, you can hand those in now and the rest later. Dialogue: “I need to talk to you about shearing these sheep!”.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Bring Fred the wool
      1. Talk to Fred the Farmer north of Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest” → “Yes, okay. I can do that” → “Yes”.
      2. Pick up the shears in Fred's house.
      3. Shear 20 wool from the sheep in the nearby field.
      4. Climb the staircase in Lumbridge Castle: the spinning wheel is on the first floor.
      5. Spin the wool into balls of wool on the spinning wheel.
      6. Climb back down the staircase. Dialogue: “Climb down the stairs”.
      7. Give Fred the balls of wool (unnoted). If you only have some of them, you can hand those in now and the rest later. Dialogue: “I need to talk to you about shearing these sheep!”.
- Quest stage table (game variable: varp 179). The game shows the row with the largest value not above the variable.
  - Value 0 and above:
      Go to: Fred the Farmer — farm north of Lumbridge (3190, 3273, plane 0), NPC Fred the Farmer
      1. Talk to Fred the Farmer north of Lumbridge to start the quest. Bring 20 balls of wool to autocomplete the quest. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest” → “Yes, okay. I can do that” → “Yes”.
         In the game list: Talk to Fred the Farmer (farm north of Lumbridge)
         - at 3190, 3273, plane 0
         - highlighted: NPC ids 732
         - key startStep
      2. Pickup the shears in Fred's house.
         In the game list: Pick up the shears in Fred's house
         - at 3190, 3273, plane 0
         - done when the bag has: Shears
         - key getSheers
      3. Shear sheep in the nearby field.
         In the game list: Shear 20 wool from the sheep
         - at 3201, 3268, plane 0
         - highlighted: NPC ids 2786
         - key shearSheep
      4. Climb the staircase in the Lumbridge Castle to spin the wool in your inventory into balls of wool.
         In the game list: Climb up Lumbridge Castle to the spinning wheel
         - at 3204, 3207, plane 0
         - key climbStairsUp
      5. Spin your wool into balls.
         In the game list: Spin the wool into balls of wool
         - at 3209, 3212, plane 1
         - highlighted: object ids 14889
         - key spinBalls
      6. Climb down the staircase. Dialogue: “Climb down the stairs”.
         In the game list: Climb down the staircase
         - at 3204, 3207, plane 1
         - highlighted: object ids 16672
         - key climbStairsDown
      7. Bring Fred the Farmer north of Lumbridge your balls of wool (UNNOTED) to progress the quest. If you only have some of the balls needed, you can still deposit them with him. Dialogue: “I need to talk to you about shearing these sheep!”.
         In the game list: Give Fred the balls of wool (unnoted)
         - at 3190, 3273, plane 0
         - highlighted: NPC ids 732
         - key turnInBalls

### S1-05 - X Marks the Spot

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: The Sheared Ram pub (Veos) (3224, 3240, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Treasure 1: by Bob's Brilliant Axes (3230, 3210, plane 0)
  - Treasure 2: behind the castle kitchen (3203, 3212, plane 0)
  - Treasure 3: by Draynor Jail (3108, 3264, plane 0)
  - Treasure 4: the Draynor pigsty (3077, 3260, plane 0)
- NPC: Veos - The Sheared Ram pub in Lumbridge, Ground floor. Dialogue: 1 → 1 ("I'm looking for a quest." → "Sounds good, what do I do?")
- Pro tip: Put the 300 XP lamp into Prayer: in F2P it is the slowest and most expensive skill. Together with The Restless Ghost, Prayer jumps straight to level 11.
- Reward: 1 QP, 200 Coins, a 300 XP lamp for any skill.
- Done when: The quest is green and the lamp has been used on Prayer.
- Items required:
  - Spade x1 - Buy one in the General Store or pick one up in the house north-east of the castle. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 952]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: Veos in the Sheared Ram pub (3228, 3242, plane 0)
  - Marked tile: Dig here (treasure 1) (3230, 3210, plane 0)
  - Marked tile: Dig here (treasure 2) (3203, 3212, plane 0)
  - Marked tile: Dig here (treasure 3) (3108, 3264, plane 0)
  - Marked tile: Dig here (treasure 4) (3077, 3260, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Veos
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Spade, Treasure scroll, Mysterious orb, Ancient casket
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm looking for a quest.” → “Sounds good, what do I do?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: X Marks the Spot
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Veos in The Sheared Ram pub in Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest” → “Sounds good, what should I do?” → “Can I help?” → “Yes”.
  2. Dig north of Bob's Brilliant Axes, on the west side of the bush by the wall of his house. Dialogue: “Okay, thanks Veos”.
  3. Dig behind Lumbridge Castle, right by the kitchen door.
  4. Dig north-west of the Draynor Village jail, by the wheat field.
  5. Dig inside the pig pen in the Draynor market.
  6. Talk to Veos directly south of the Rusty Anchor Inn in Port Sarim to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Talk to Veos
      1. Talk to Veos in The Sheared Ram pub in Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest” → “Sounds good, what should I do?” → “Can I help?” → “Yes”.
  - Solve the clue scroll
      1. Dig north of Bob's Brilliant Axes, on the west side of the bush by the wall of his house. Dialogue: “Okay, thanks Veos”.
      2. Dig behind Lumbridge Castle, right by the kitchen door.
      3. Dig north-west of the Draynor Village jail, by the wheat field.
      4. Dig inside the pig pen in the Draynor market.
  - Bring the casket to Veos
      1. Talk to Veos directly south of the Rusty Anchor Inn in Port Sarim to finish the quest.

### S1-06 - The Restless Ghost

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Lumbridge church (Father Aereck) (3243, 3206, plane 0)
- NPC: Father Aereck - Lumbridge church, Ground floor. Dialogue: 3 → 1 ("I'm looking for a quest!" → "Ok, let me help then.")
- Reward: 1 QP, 1,125 Prayer XP: from level 1 that is Prayer level 9, and together with the lamp from X Marks the Spot it is level 11.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Ghostspeak amulet x1 - Father Urhney gives it to you in his shack in Lumbridge Swamp. Put it around your neck. [from: Father Urhney; obtained during the step; item id 552]
- Items recommended:
  - Cooked chicken x5 - Cross the bridge east of Lumbridge Castle and head north along the river to the farm's chicken coop (Lumbridge East Farm). Kill chickens (level 1, they barely hit), pick up the Raw chicken and cook it on the castle kitchen's range (Cook's range): after Cook's Assistant food burns less often there. Bring a couple more chickens than you need: at Cooking level 1 some of it will burn. Pick up the Feathers too. About 65 gp on the Grand Exchange. [heals 3; item id 2140]
  - Shrimps x5 - Instead of chicken: catch fish with a Small fishing net on the shore of Lumbridge Swamp by the Fishing tutor (on the Lumbridge Swamp shore by the Fishing tutor) and cook them on the castle range or on a fire (axe + tinderbox). About 23 gp on the Grand Exchange. [heals 3; item id 315]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: Father Aereck, Lumbridge church (3243, 3206, plane 0)
  - Stop 1: Father Aereck in the church (3243, 3210, plane 0)
  - Stop 2: Father Urhney in his shack in the swamp (3148, 3175, plane 0)
  - Stop 3: The coffin in the graveyard by the church (3250, 3193, plane 0)
  - Stop 4: Wizards' Tower — go down to the basement altar (3109, 3160, plane 0)
  - Stop 5: Put the skull back in the coffin (3250, 3193, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Father Aereck, Father Urhney, Restless ghost, Skeleton
  - Objects highlighted: Coffin, Altar
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Ghostspeak amulet, Ghost's skull, Cooked chicken, Shrimps
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm looking for a quest!” → “Ok, let me help then.” → “Father Aereck sent me to talk to you.” → “He's got a ghost haunting his graveyard.” → “Yep, now tell me what the problem is.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: The Restless Ghost
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Father Aereck in the Lumbridge Church. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest!” → “Yes”.
  2. Talk to Father Urhney in the hut west of Lumbridge Swamp. Dialogue: “Father Aereck sent me to talk to you” → “He's got a ghost haunting his graveyard”.
  3. Open the coffin in the Lumbridge graveyard to spawn the ghost.
  4. Search the coffin in the Lumbridge graveyard to spawn the ghost.
  5. Talk to the ghost while wearing the ghostspeak amulet. Dialogue: “Yep, now tell me what the problem is” → “Yep, clever aren't I?” → “Yes, ok. Do you know WHY you're a ghost?” → “Yes, ok. Do you know why you're a ghost?”.
  6. Enter the Wizards' Tower basement, south of Draynor Village.
  7. Search the altar. A skeleton (level 13) appears and attacks you, but you can just run away.
  8. Leave the basement.
  9. Open the ghost's coffin in the Lumbridge graveyard.
  10. Search the coffin to put the skull back and finish the quest.

      3. Talk to the ghost while wearing the ghostspeak amulet. Dialogue: “Yep, now tell me what the problem is” → “Yep, clever aren't I?” → “Yes, ok. Do you know WHY you're a ghost?” → “Yes, ok. Do you know why you're a ghost?”.
  - Return the skull
      1. Enter the Wizards' Tower basement, south of Draynor Village.
      2. Search the altar. A skeleton (level 13) appears and attacks you, but you can just run away.
      3. Leave the basement.

      5. Search the coffin to put the skull back and finish the quest.

### S1-07 - Misthalin Mystery

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Abigale by the boat — south-east Lumbridge Swamp (3236, 3155, plane 0)
- NPC: Abigale - The south-east corner of Lumbridge Swamp, by the fishing spots, Ground floor. Dialogue: 1 → 1 ("What has happened here?" → "What do you want me to do?")
- Notes: A detective quest in a manor, with no combat. You will find the bucket, tinderbox and knife during the quest. Follow the Quest Helper hints.
- Reward: 1 QP, 600 Crafting XP, uncut ruby, emerald and sapphire (sell them on the Grand Exchange).
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Use Bucket on Barrel of rainwater
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: Abigale by the boat in the swamp (3236, 3155, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Abigale
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “What has happened here?” → “What do you want me to do?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Misthalin Mystery
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Abigale in the south-east corner of Lumbridge Swamp. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Board the rowboat south of Abigale; it takes you to the manor.
  3. Pick up the bucket near the fountain.
  4. Search the barrel of rainwater north of the fountain to trigger a cutscene.
  5. Use the bucket on the barrel of rainwater.
  6. Search the barrel again: the manor key is at the bottom.
  7. Enter the manor.
  8. Take the knife from the table.
  9. Try to open the door with the pink handle.
  10. Pick up the note that appeared.
  11. Read the note.
  12. Use the knife on the marked painting.
  13. Search the painting for the ruby key.
  14. Go through the door with the ruby handle.
  15. Search the shelves for a tinderbox.
  16. Light all the unlit candles in the room.
  17. Light the fuse on the barrel of gunpowder.
  18. Leave the room to trigger the explosion.
  19. Climb over the damaged wall.
  20. Observe Lacey through the trees.
  21. Pick up the note by the fence.
  22. Read the note.
  23. Search the piano (right-click Search) for the emerald key. The tune is D-E-A-D.
  24. Climb back over the damaged wall into the manor.
  25. Go through the door with the green handle.
  26. Try to enter the room containing the Bandos godsword; another murder takes place.
  27. Pick up the note by the door.
  28. Read the note.
  29. Use the knife on the unlit fireplace in the eastern room.
  30. Search the fireplace and enter the stones in order: Sapphire, Diamond, Zenyte, Emerald, Onyx, Ruby.
  31. Search the fireplace again for the sapphire key.
  32. Go through the door with the sapphire handle.
  33. Move the mirror to reflect the knives the murderer throws; a black swirl surrounds the wardrobe he throws from.
  34. Watch the killer's reveal cutscene.
  35. Pick up the killer's knife.
  36. Equip the killer's knife, then select Fight on Abigale (no actual combat occurs).
  37. Attempt to go through the sapphire door.
  38. Talk to Mandy just outside the manor to complete the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Abigale
      1. Talk to Abigale in the south-east corner of Lumbridge Swamp. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  - Enter the manor
      1. Board the rowboat south of Abigale; it takes you to the manor.
      2. Pick up the bucket near the fountain.
      3. Search the barrel of rainwater north of the fountain to trigger a cutscene.
      4. Use the bucket on the barrel of rainwater.
      5. Search the barrel again: the manor key is at the bottom.
      6. Enter the manor.
  - Puzzle 1
      1. Take the knife from the table.
      2. Try to open the door with the pink handle.
      3. Pick up the note that appeared.
      4. Read the note.
      5. Use the knife on the marked painting.
      6. Search the painting for the ruby key.
      7. Go through the door with the ruby handle.
  - Puzzle 2
      1. Search the shelves for a tinderbox.
      2. Light all the unlit candles in the room.
      3. Light the fuse on the barrel of gunpowder.
      4. Leave the room to trigger the explosion.
      5. Climb over the damaged wall.
  - Puzzle 3
      1. Observe Lacey through the trees.
      2. Pick up the note by the fence.
      3. Read the note.
      4. Search the piano (right-click Search) for the emerald key. The tune is D-E-A-D.
  - Another murder
      1. Climb back over the damaged wall into the manor.
      2. Go through the door with the green handle.
      3. Try to enter the room containing the Bandos godsword; another murder takes place.
  - Puzzle 4
      1. Pick up the note by the door.
      2. Read the note.
      3. Use the knife on the unlit fireplace in the eastern room.
      4. Search the fireplace and enter the stones in order: Sapphire, Diamond, Zenyte, Emerald, Onyx, Ruby.
      5. Search the fireplace again for the sapphire key.
  - The murderer
      1. Go through the door with the sapphire handle.
      2. Move the mirror to reflect the knives the murderer throws; a black swirl surrounds the wardrobe he throws from.
      3. Watch the killer's reveal cutscene.
      4. Pick up the killer's knife.
      5. Equip the killer's knife, then select Fight on Abigale (no actual combat occurs).
      6. Attempt to go through the sapphire door.
      7. Talk to Mandy just outside the manor to complete the quest.

### S1-08 - Woodcutting to 15 and Firemaking to 15

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type skill
- Where: Ordinary trees (Tree) right behind the west wall of Lumbridge Castle: leave the castle gate to the west and walk 15-20 tiles to the grove. Chop and burn the logs right away; no bank is needed.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The trees behind the castle's west wall (3190, 3222, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - The trees behind the castle's west wall (3190, 3222, plane 0) - Ordinary trees (Tree) 15-20 tiles west of the castle gate. Use only them up to Woodcutting 15; oaks (Oak) start at 15.
  - The canoe station on the River Lum (3241, 3235, plane 0) - The east bank of the River Lum, about 10 tiles north of the bridge by the castle. You need an axe: in the bag, in your hands or handed to Barfy Bill (Store-axe).
- Canoe: Your Woodcutting level decides how many stops you can paddle: 12 - one (Lumbridge to Champions' Guild), 27 - two (to Barbarian Village), 42 - three (to Edgeville). River stops: Lumbridge, Champions' Guild, Barbarian Village, Edgeville. A canoe disappears after the trip, so you chop a new one for every trip. How to travel step by step is in the reference [Teleports, canoes and boats](#/reference/transport).
- Faster: Up to 15, chop ordinary trees (Tree) by the castle and burn the logs at once with the tinderbox. From level 15 move on to oaks (Oak).
- Keep: 1 Ashes in the bank for Prince Ali Rescue. Ashes stay on the ground when a fire burns out.
- Done when: Woodcutting 15, Firemaking 15 and 1 Ashes in the bank (for S2-10).
- Pace: woodcutting to level 15 (2,411 XP): log|logs, 25 XP each
- Items required:
  - Bronze axe x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, buy one from Bob's Brilliant Axes in south Lumbridge for 16 gp. [from: Bob; item id 1351]
  - Tinderbox x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. If you lost it, buy one in the Lumbridge General Store for 1 gp. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 590]
- Keep for S2-10: Ashes x1 (bank) - Prince Ali Rescue needs ashes for the wig dye.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: The trees behind the castle's west wall (3190, 3222, plane 0)
  - Objects highlighted: Tree, Oak tree, Canoe Station
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Bronze axe, Tinderbox, Logs, Ashes
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: woodcutting 15, firemaking 15 and holding 1 x Ashes
- Walkthrough:
  1. Leave Lumbridge Castle by the west gate and chop ordinary trees (Tree) in the grove behind the wall.
  2. Burn every log at once: use the tinderbox (Tinderbox) on the logs (Logs). Move along the row of trees: you cannot light a new fire on the spot of an old one.
  3. Pick up 1 Ashes after a fire and put it in the bank: it is needed for the paste in Prince Ali Rescue (S2-10).
  4. From Woodcutting 15 switch to oaks (Oak) in the same place, and from Firemaking 15 burn oak logs.
  5. From Woodcutting 12 try the canoe: the station is on the east bank of the River Lum, about 10 tiles north of the bridge by the castle. "Chop-down" fells the tree, "Shape-Canoe" picks the Log canoe, "Float Log" launches it, "Paddle Log" chooses the stop.

### S1-09 - Stronghold of Security: starting capital of 10,000 Coins

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type prep
- Where: A hole in the ground (Entrance) in the middle of Barbarian Village, by the mine, north of Draynor and west of Varrock.
- Map: Stronghold of Security entrance, Barbarian Village (3081, 3420, plane 0)
- Warning: First turn on two-factor authentication on your Jagex Account: without it the money chests will not open. From the 2nd floor the monsters are aggressive and attack on their own: keep food at hand. Eat while your health is still more than two of the biggest hits of the monster next to you, not when it is already low. The deeper floors hit much harder than floor 1, so eat earlier there.
- Where to heal: Eat in the safe corridor between the doors. The Box of Health chest on the 3rd floor fully restores your health. If you run out of food, go back up: health regenerates by itself, 1 point a minute.
- Monsters: Floor 1: goblins and minotaurs up to level 27 (a multicombat area), floor 2: zombies up to 53, floor 3: spiders and catablepons up to 68, floor 4: ankou and shades. From floor 2 they attack on their own, so do not stop.
- Rewards: Floor 1 (Gift of Peace): 2,000 coins, floor 2 (Grain of Plenty): 3,000, floor 3 (Box of Health): 5,000, floor 4 (Cradle of Life): Fighting boots or Fancy boots. Together that is 10,000 coins. Each chest opens once.
- Reward: 10,000 Coins (2,000 + 3,000 + 5,000), boots (Fancy boots or Fighting boots) and 4 emotes.
- Done when: Fighting boots or Fancy boots in the bag, worn or in the bank: the reward of the 4th chest, so all four levels are done. The coins may be spent already.
- Items recommended:
  - Cooked chicken x15–20 - Cross the bridge east of Lumbridge Castle and head north along the river to the farm's chicken coop (Lumbridge East Farm). Kill chickens (level 1, they barely hit), pick up the Raw chicken and cook it on the castle kitchen's range (Cook's range): after Cook's Assistant food burns less often there. Bring a couple more chickens than you need: at Cooking level 1 some of it will burn. Pick up the Feathers too. About 65 gp on the Grand Exchange. [heals 3; item id 2140]
  - Shrimps x15–20 - Instead of chicken: catch fish with a Small fishing net on the shore of Lumbridge Swamp by the Fishing tutor (on the Lumbridge Swamp shore by the Fishing tutor) and cook them on the castle range or on a fire (axe + tinderbox). About 23 gp on the Grand Exchange. [heals 3; item id 315]
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Canoe to Champions' Guild [canoe-log] - needs woodcutting 12
      also: any axe - 1 of Bronze axe, Iron axe, Steel axe, Black axe, Mithril axe, Adamant axe, Rune axe, Dragon axe
      goes to: The Lumbridge canoe station (Barfy Bill) (3243, 3238, plane 0)
      instead: At the Lumbridge canoe station (Barfy Bill, the east bank of the Lum north of the bridge) make a **Log canoe**: "Chop-down", "Shape-Canoe", "Float Log", "Paddle Log". You need an axe. The canoe goes one stop, to Champions' Guild (Tarquin); from there walk north-west to Barbarian Village.
  - Canoe straight to Barbarian Village [canoe-dugout] - needs woodcutting 27
      also: any axe - 1 of Bronze axe, Iron axe, Steel axe, Black axe, Mithril axe, Adamant axe, Rune axe, Dragon axe
      goes to: The Lumbridge canoe station (Barfy Bill) (3243, 3238, plane 0)
      instead: A **Dugout canoe** goes two stops, straight to the Barbarian Village station (Sigurd); from there it is about 30 tiles west to the Stronghold entrance. The actions are the same, only "Float Canoe" and "Paddle Canoe".
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: The Stronghold of Security entrance (3081, 3420, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Count Check
  - Objects highlighted: Entrance, Gift of Peace, Grain of Plenty, Box of Health, Cradle of Life
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Cooked chicken, Shrimps
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 1 x Fighting boots, Fancy boots
- Walkthrough:
  1. The fastest way: talk to Count Check in the Lumbridge graveyard, south of the church: Talk-to, "Where can I learn more about security?", "Yes". He teleports you for free straight to the entrance, but only once and only if you have never been to the Stronghold.
  2. Without the teleport: a canoe at Woodcutting 12-26 takes you from Lumbridge only as far as Champions' Guild (1 stop), then walk north-west along the river, about 2 minutes. At Woodcutting 27 the canoe goes straight to Barbarian Village.
  3. Find the hole (Entrance) in the middle of the village by the mine and climb down.
  4. Turn on run (the boot icon by the minimap) and do not attack the monsters: run from door to door.
  5. The doors come in pairs. Between them is an empty corridor where nobody hits you: eat and recover here. At the second door there is sometimes a security question: the right answer is always that you never share your password, PIN or codes and do not trust links in chat.
  6. Floor 1 (Vault of War): at the end open Gift of Peace for 2,000 coins. Then take the ladder down.
  7. Floor 2 (Catacomb of Famine): the monsters are aggressive here, so do not linger. At the end open Grain of Plenty for 3,000 coins.
  8. Floor 3 (Pit of Pestilence): open the Box of Health for 5,000 coins and a full health restore.
  9. Floor 4 (Sepulchre of Death): open the Cradle of Life and choose Fighting boots or Fancy boots.
  10. The way out is the home teleport: Magic tab, Lumbridge Home Teleport (the first icon), and do not press anything for about 14 seconds. It is free, once every 30 minutes; if the icon is grey the teleport is recharging, so go back by the ladders (Climb-up).

### S1-10 - Buy the Chronicle book in Draynor Village

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type prep
- Map: The Draynor Village market square (Diango) (3082, 3248, plane 0)
- NPC: Diango - The Draynor Village market square, Ground floor
- Why: The book takes you to Champions' Guild at the south edge of Varrock in a couple of seconds, from where the Grand Exchange is a couple of minutes' walk. The main fast F2P transport until Varrock Teleport (Magic 25).
- Budget: About 1,900 gp: the book 300, each card from 150 (Diango's price rises if you buy many at once). The money comes from Stronghold of Security (S1-09).
- Done when: The Chronicle is charged with 10 cards and is in your inventory.
- Use Teleport card on Chronicle (item)
- Items required:
  - Chronicle x1 - Buy one from Diango (the toy shop in the Draynor Village market) for 300 gp. [from: Diango; obtained during the step; item id 13660]
  - Teleport card x10 - Buy from Diango from 150 gp each and use them on the book: it holds up to 1,000 charges. [from: Diango; obtained during the step; item id 13658]
  - Coins x1800 - The book 300 and ten cards from 150 each (Diango's price rises when you buy many at once). The money comes from Stronghold of Security (S1-09). [item id 995]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Diango
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Chronicle, Teleport card
  - No automatic completion: you mark this step yourself.
- Walkthrough:
  1. Go to the Draynor Village market and find Diango: right-click him, Trade.
  2. Buy the Chronicle book (300 gp) and 10 Teleport cards (from 150 gp).
  3. Charge the book: pick a card (Use) and click the book. All the cards in your bag are used: 10 cards give 10 teleports.
  4. To teleport, right-click the book, Teleport. A plain click does not teleport; it wields the book in the shield slot.
  5. To see how many charges are left, right-click, Check charges. When the book is better and when a canoe or Varrock Teleport is, see the reference [Teleports, canoes and boats](#/reference/transport).

### S1-12 - Mining to 10

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type skill
- Where: The mine south-east of Varrock (South-east Varrock mine): copper, tin and iron in one place, the Varrock East bank is about 60 tiles north-west. From the Chronicle book (Champions' Guild) it is about 1 minute on foot to the east. On the way keep south of the stone circle with the dark wizards (Dark wizard): they attack on their own and hit beginners hard.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The mine south-east of Varrock (3286, 3365, plane 0)
- How: Up to 10 mine copper (Copper) and tin (Tin) and drop the extra (Shift + click).
- Why: The Knight's Sword (S2-07) requires Mining 10. The ore for Doric's Quest is bought in S2-01, so no iron is mined here.
- Put in the bank: 1 copper and 1 tin ore to smelt a bronze bar for Prince Ali Rescue.
- Done when: Mining 10, with 1 copper ore and 1 tin ore in the bank (for S2-10).
- Pace: mining to level 10 (1,154 XP): ore, 17.5 XP each
- Items required:
  - Bronze pickaxe x1 - Given on Tutorial Island. A better pickaxe (Steel pickaxe) mines faster. [from: Bob; item id 1265]
- Keep for S2-10: Copper ore x1 (bank) - Smelted with a tin ore into the Bronze bar of Prince Ali Rescue.
- Keep for S2-10: Tin ore x1 (bank) - Smelted with a copper ore into the Bronze bar of Prince Ali Rescue.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Objects highlighted: Copper rocks, Tin rocks, Iron rocks
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: mining 10 and holding 1 x Copper ore; 1 x Tin ore

### S1-13 - Money for the shopping: cowhides

- stage 1 (Lumbridge, fast transport and the first quests); type prep
- Where: The cow field north-east of Lumbridge Castle, across the river (Lumbridge cow field).
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The cow field east of Lumbridge (3257, 3272, plane 0)
- How: Kill cows (level 2) and pick up the hides (Cowhide). A full inventory is 28 hides. The bank is in Lumbridge Castle (2nd floor), or sell straight on the Grand Exchange.
- How much you need: The S2-01 shopping costs about 10,500 gp. After Stronghold and Chronicle you have about 8,500: top it up with 40-60 hides and sell them. The Magic training in S2-04 pays for itself with the hides of the cows you train on.
- Price: A hide is about 110 gp on the Grand Exchange (prices of 26 Sep 2026): 28 hides are about 3,000 gp. Leather costs about 260 gp: after Prince Ali Rescue take the hides to the tanner Ellis in Al Kharid (1 gp each), which earns about twice as much.
- Bonus: Attack, Strength and Defence rise as well, and cows drop bones: bury them for Prayer.
- New account: A new free-to-play account cannot sell cowhides or leather on the Grand Exchange until it has 20 hours of play, 10 quest points and total level 100. Then the hides are not money: after Stronghold and the Chronicle you have about 8,500 gp, which already covers the S2-01 quest materials, the Imp Catcher beads and the first scimitars (about 8,000 gp). Skip the hide grind, kill cows only for combat levels and bones, and let the amulet, the potions and the mithril scimitar wait for money from quests (Below Ice Mountain gives 2,000) or from items that can be sold.
- Money goal: 12,000 coins in the bag and the bank by the end of the step
- Done when: 12,000+ coins in the bag or the bank (together with what is left from Stronghold). Unsold hides are not coins.
- Fights in melee: Cow
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Cow
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Cowhide
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 12000 x Coins
- sells: ["Cowhide","Leather"]
- moneyGoalNoSale: 8000

## Stage 2 - Varrock, teleport magic and key quests

### S2-01 - Big shopping on the Grand Exchange

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type gear
- Map: The Grand Exchange (3164, 3487, plane 0)
- How: Teleport with the Chronicle book (right-click the book, Teleport) to Champions' Guild and go north to the Grand Exchange, away from the stone circle of dark wizards to the north-east. Sell the hides first, then buy. The prices are approximate, of 26 Sep 2026; the inspector (click an item) shows the exact price.
- Budget: About 10,500 gp, of which about 5,300 is the beads for Imp Catcher. To save: buy the pink skirt from Thessalia in Varrock for 2 gp and make the dyes with the witch Aggie in Draynor (5 gp + 2 onions, 3 redberries or 2 Woad leaves).
- New account: A new free-to-play account cannot sell cowhides (or leather, iron ore, clay, copper or tin ore, coal, steel bars, oak or yew logs, lobsters, ashes, feathers, the low runes and a few more) on the Grand Exchange until it has 20 hours of play, 10 quest points and total level 100; buying is not restricted. Without selling, buy in this order: the quest materials (about 2,400 gp), then the Imp Catcher beads and the iron and steel scimitars (about 5,400 gp), then, as coins allow, the mithril scimitar, the amulet of strength and the energy potions. Do not skip the first two groups for the third.
- Done when: All the items are bought and are in your Grand Exchange bank.
- Items required:
  - Red bead x1 - For Imp Catcher, about 2,000 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1470]
  - Yellow bead x1 - For Imp Catcher, about 900 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1472]
  - Black bead x1 - For Imp Catcher, about 1,200 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1474]
  - White bead x1 - For Imp Catcher, about 1,150 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1476]
  - Amulet of strength x1 - +10 strength bonus: you hit harder and melee levels much faster. About 1,350 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1725]
  - Iron scimitar x1 - Can be worn at once. About 60 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1323]
  - Steel scimitar x1 - Wear it from Attack 5. About 100 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1325]
  - Mithril scimitar x1 - Wear it from Attack 20, after Vampyre Slayer. About 400 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1329]
  - Energy potion(4) x5 - Restores run energy during quests, about 170 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 3008]
  - Redberry pie x1 - For The Knight's Sword, about 70 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 2325]
  - Iron bar x2 - For The Knight's Sword, about 200 gp each. [obtained during the step; item id 2351]
  - Clay x7 - 6 for Doric's Quest, 1 to soak in water for Prince Ali Rescue. About 80 gp each. [obtained during the step; item id 434]
  - Copper ore x4 - For Doric's Quest, about 30 gp each. [obtained during the step; item id 436]
  - Iron ore x2 - For Doric's Quest, about 70 gp each. [obtained during the step; item id 440]
  - Redberries x1 - For the paste in Prince Ali Rescue, about 50 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1951]
  - Yellow dye x2 - One for the wig in Prince Ali Rescue, the other for the orange dye in Goblin Diplomacy. About 230 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1765]
  - Red dye x1 - Mix with yellow to make orange for Goblin Diplomacy, about 290 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1763]
  - Blue dye x1 - For Goblin Diplomacy, about 290 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1767]
  - Pink skirt x1 - For Prince Ali Rescue. Cheapest from Thessalia in Varrock for 2 gp. [from: Thessalia; obtained during the step; item id 1013]
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Teleport with the Chronicle book [chronicle] - needs item Chronicle
      goes to: The Champions' Guild exit (Chronicle) (3201, 3355, plane 0)
      instead: Right-click the Chronicle book, Teleport (uses 1 charge): you arrive at Champions' Guild, and from there walk north through Varrock to the Exchange. Avoid the stone circle with the dark wizards to the north-east.

### S2-03 - Witch's Potion

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Witch Hetty's house in Rimmington (2968, 3204, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:

- Use Raw rat meat on Fireplace

  - Onion x1 - Pick one in the patch north of Rimmington. [obtained during the step; item id 1957]
  - Eye of newt x1 - Buy one in Betty's shop in Port Sarim for 3 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 221]
  - Rat's tail x1 - Kill a rat in the shop Brian's Archery Supplies, west of Hetty's house: the tail drops only after the quest has started. [obtained during the step; item id 300]
  - Burnt meat x1 - Raw rat meat from a giant rat by the Port Sarim chapel (or Raw beef): use it on Hetty's fireplace twice, until it burns. [obtained during the step; item id 2146]

- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Hetty in her house in Rimmington. Dialogue: “I am in search of a quest” → “Yes”.
  2. Kill a rat in the house to the west for a rat's tail. The tail only drops after you start the quest.
  3. Buy an eye of newt from Betty's Magic Emporium in Port Sarim (3 gp).
  4. Get burnt meat: kill a giant rat by the Port Sarim chapel, take its raw rat meat (raw beef works too) and cook it twice on the fireplace in Hetty's house until it burns.
  5. Pick an onion in the field north of Rimmington.
  6. Bring all four ingredients to Hetty.
  7. Drink from the cauldron in Hetty's house to finish the quest.

### S2-04 - Magic to 25 (Varrock Teleport)

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type skill
- Map: The cow field east of Lumbridge (3257, 3272, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - The cow field — hit cows, every one drops a hide (3257, 3272, plane 0) [NPC Cow]
  - Aubury's Rune Shop — Air and Mind runes (3253, 3402, plane 0) [NPC Aubury] [items: Air rune, Mind rune]
  - Zaff's Superior Staffs — the staff (3203, 3434, plane 0) [NPC Zaff] [items: Staff]
- How: After Imp Catcher (875 XP) and Witch's Potion (325) you have Magic 10 without a single spell. After that, cast combat strike spells on the cows in Lumbridge: a cow has 8 health and every one drops a hide, so the XP comes together with money. Spells of the same tier hit equally hard and equally accurately; they differ in XP and runes: Wind Strike (Magic 1) 5.5 XP, Water Strike (5) 7.5, Earth Strike (9) 9.5, Fire Strike (13) 11.5. The XP goes to both Magic and Hitpoints.
- Why: Varrock Teleport opens at level 25: Magic tab, Varrock Teleport, costing 1 Law, 3 Air and 1 Fire rune each, and a couple of seconds later you are on the square in the middle of Varrock. When it beats the book and the canoe, see the reference [Teleports, canoes and boats](#/reference/transport).
- Budget: The exact calculation is in the "How much it costs to reach Magic 25" block below: it takes your XP from the game and the Grand Exchange prices. The most expensive is Fire Strike without a staff: 3 fire runes, 2 air and 1 mind per cast.
- If you are short of money: Start with what you have: a couple of hundred runes and an ordinary Staff cost less than 1,500 gp. The hides from the cows you train on pay the costs back: a hide is about 120 gp on the Grand Exchange. After that comes the "How to make up the money" block below with tips for your levels.
- Done when: Magic 25.
- Shows the magic gear advice for your levels and coins
- Shows the cost of reaching Magic 25 with combat spells (from 10)
- Items required:
  - Staff x1 - Buy from Zaff in Zaff's Superior Staffs (Varrock) for about 15 gp: with a staff the spell can be set to autocast. A Staff of fire (below) works instead. [item id 1379]
  - Air rune x300+ - Needed for every cast (1-2 each). In Aubury's Rune Shop (Varrock) it is 4 gp, about 6 gp on the Grand Exchange. Take 300 to start, buy more later with the hide money. [item id 556]
  - Mind rune x300+ - Needed for every cast (1 each). In Aubury's Rune Shop it is 3 gp. Take 300 to start. [item id 558]
- Items recommended:
  - Staff of fire x1 - Instead of an ordinary Staff: it replaces the fire runes for Fire Strike (3 runes per cast). In Zaff's Superior Staffs about 1,500 gp, about 940 gp on the Grand Exchange. It pays for itself: see the calculation below. [item id 1387]
  - Earth rune x100+ - Earth Strike (Magic 9-12) uses 2 earth runes per cast. If you go straight to Fire Strike you do not need it. [item id 557]
  - Fire rune x300+ - Fire Strike (from Magic 13) uses 3 fire runes per cast, unless you have a Staff of fire. [item id 554]
  - Law rune x10+ - For the teleports once you have the level: one Law rune with 3 Air runes and 1 Fire rune per Varrock Teleport (Magic 25), and again for Lumbridge (31) and Falador (37). Buy them at the Grand Exchange on your next stop in Varrock; they are not needed for training. [item id 563]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Cow
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Cowhide
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: magic 25
- Walkthrough:
  1. Buy Air and Mind runes in Aubury's Rune Shop in Varrock (300 each to start): in the shop they are 4 and 3 gp, Air is about 6 gp on the Grand Exchange.
  2. Buy a staff from Zaff in Zaff's Superior Staffs (Varrock): an ordinary Staff is about 15 gp, which is enough to autocast a spell. A Staff of fire costs more but replaces the fire runes.
  3. Wield the staff, open the combat tab (Combat Options), click the spell button and choose the autocast spell: Wind Strike first.
  4. Go to the cow field near Lumbridge and hit the cows: each has 8 health, and a hide (Cowhide) always drops. Pick up the hides; a full inventory goes to the bank or the exchange.
  5. Change the autocast spell with your level: Magic 5 Water Strike, 9 Earth Strike, 13 Fire Strike. Fire Strike gives the most XP per cast.
  6. Continue to Magic 25: Varrock Teleport unlocks. Buy more runes with the money from the hides.

  8. Talk to Romeo in Varrock Square to finish the quest.

### S2-07 - The Knight's Sword

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 1 quest point
- Comes after: S2-01
- Requirements:
  - mining 10 (needed during the quest; you can start without it) (can be boosted)
- Map: Squire, White Knights' Castle (2978, 3341, plane 0)
- NPC: Squire - The courtyard of White Knights' Castle in Falador, Ground floor
- Requirement: Mining 10: you have it after step S1-12.
- Dangerous: Blurite ore is mined in the Asgarnian Ice Dungeon: the entrance is at Mudskipper Point south of Port Sarim. There are ice giants (level 53) and ice warriors (level 57): run in, mine the ore and leave at once. Eat a trout before your health falls to two hits of the monster next to you; ice giants hit hard.
- Reward: 1 QP and 12,725 Smithing XP: the skill jumps instantly from 1 straight to level 29 without smelting by hand!
- Done when: The quest is green, Smithing 29.
- Items required:
  - Redberry pie x1 - From step S2-01. [item id 2325]
  - Iron bar x2 - From step S2-01. [item id 2351]
  - Bronze pickaxe x1 - Any pickaxe from the bank: the bronze one from Tutorial Island will do. [item id 1265]
  - Blurite ore x1 - Mine it in the Asgarnian Ice Dungeon at Mudskipper Point. [from: Asgarnian Ice Dungeon; obtained during the step; item id 668]
- Items recommended:
  - Trout x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 24 gp each. Or 8 Salmon instead (about 30 gp each): to catch them yourself you need Fishing 30 and Cooking 25, fly fishing in the same place as the trout (Barbarian Village). [any of: Salmon; heals 7; item id 333]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Squire, Reldo, Thurgo, Sir Vyvin
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Redberry pie, Iron bar, Blurite ore, Portrait, Blurite sword
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “And how is life as a squire?” → “I can make a new sword if you like...” → “So would these dwarves make another one?” → “Ok, I'll give it a go.” → “What do you know about the Imcando dwarfs?” → “Would you like a redberry pie?” → “Something else.” → “About that sword...” → “Can you make that replacement sword now?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: The Knight's Sword
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to the Squire in the courtyard of Falador Castle. Dialogue: “And how is life as a squire?” → “I can make a new sword if you like” → “So would these dwarves make another one?” → “Yes”.
  2. Talk to Reldo in the library of Varrock Palace. Dialogue: “What do you know about the Imcando dwarves?”.
  3. Talk to Thurgo south of Port Sarim and give him a redberry pie. Dialogue: “Would you like a redberry pie?”.
  4. Talk to Thurgo again. Dialogue: “Can you make a special sword for me?”.
  5. Talk to the Squire in the courtyard of Falador Castle.
  6. Climb up the east ladder in Falador Castle.
  7. Go up the staircase west of the ladder on the first floor.
  8. Search the cupboard in the room south of the staircase. Sir Vyvin must be in the other room, or he will notice you.
  9. Bring Thurgo the portrait. Dialogue: “About that sword”.
  10. Go down the ladder south of Port Sarim into the Asgarnian Ice Dungeon. Ice giants and ice warriors attack here, so keep away from them.
  11. Mine a blurite ore in the eastern cavern.
  12. Return to Thurgo with a blurite ore and two iron bars. Dialogue: “Can you make that replacement sword now?”.
  13. Return to the Squire with the sword to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to the Squire in the courtyard of Falador Castle. Dialogue: “And how is life as a squire?” → “I can make a new sword if you like” → “So would these dwarves make another one?” → “Yes”.
      2. Talk to Reldo in the library of Varrock Palace. Dialogue: “What do you know about the Imcando dwarves?”.
  - Find the Imcando dwarf
      1. Talk to Thurgo south of Port Sarim and give him a redberry pie. Dialogue: “Would you like a redberry pie?”.
      2. Talk to Thurgo again. Dialogue: “Can you make a special sword for me?”.
  - Find the portrait
      1. Talk to the Squire in the courtyard of Falador Castle.
      2. Climb up the east ladder in Falador Castle.
      3. Go up the staircase west of the ladder on the first floor.
      4. Search the cupboard in the room south of the staircase. Sir Vyvin must be in the other room, or he will notice you.
      5. Bring Thurgo the portrait. Dialogue: “About that sword”.
  - Forge the sword
      1. Go down the ladder south of Port Sarim into the Asgarnian Ice Dungeon. Ice giants and ice warriors attack here, so keep away from them.
      2. Mine a blurite ore in the eastern cavern.
      3. Return to Thurgo with a blurite ore and two iron bars. Dialogue: “Can you make that replacement sword now?”.
  - Return the sword
      1. Return to the Squire with the sword to finish the quest.

### S2-08 - Vampyre Slayer

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 3 quest points
- Map: Morgan's house in Draynor Village (3098, 3268, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Garlic — the cupboard on the 1st floor of Morgan's house (3096, 3270, plane 1) [items: Garlic]
- NPC: Morgan - A house in Draynor Village, Ground floor
- Combat: Count Draynor, level 34.
- Safespot: Garlic in your inventory weakens the vampire. Stand behind the chair (safespot) and hit him with Fire Strike, or fight him in melee with a sword, healing with trout (+7) or salmon (+9).
- Reward: 3 QP and 4,825 Attack XP: your Attack level jumps instantly from 1 to 20!
- Done when: The quest is green and Attack is 20 or more.
- Fights in melee: Count Draynor
- Picture: A safespot against Count Draynor
- Items required:
  - Hammer x1 - Buy from the General Store north of the Blue Moon Inn pub in Varrock for 1 gp. [from: Varrock General Store; item id 2347]
  - Stake x1 - Dr Harlow gives it to you in the Blue Moon Inn pub: treat him to a beer. [from: Dr Harlow; obtained during the step; item id 1549]
  - Beer x1 - Buy from the Blue Moon Inn bartender for 2 gp. [from: Dr Harlow; obtained during the step; item id 1917]
  - Garlic x1 - From the cupboard on the 1st floor of Morgan's house. [obtained during the step; item id 1550]
- Items recommended:
  - Trout x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 24 gp each. Or 8 Salmon instead (about 30 gp each): to catch them yourself you need Fishing 30 and Cooking 25, fly fishing in the same place as the trout (Barbarian Village). [any of: Salmon; heals 7; item id 333]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Wield the Mithril scimitar bought in S2-01: Attack 20 is reached with this quest. [checked from the worn items: Mithril scimitar, Adamant scimitar, Rune scimitar, Dragon scimitar]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: Morgan's house in Draynor Village (3098, 3268, plane 0)
  - NPCs highlighted: Morgan, Dr Harlow, Count Draynor
  - Objects highlighted: Coffin
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Stake, Hammer, Garlic, Beer, Trout, Salmon
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yes” → “I need your help dealing with a vampyre.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Vampyre Slayer
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Morgan in the north of Draynor Village. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Take the garlic from the cupboard upstairs in Morgan's house. Garlic is optional (mark it off in Quest Helper's panel), but it weakens the vampyre.
  3. Talk to Dr. Harlow in the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock. Dialogue: “Morgan needs your help!”.
  4. Buy a beer from the bartender in the Blue Moon Inn. Dialogue: “A glass of your finest ale please”.
  5. Talk to Dr. Harlow again and give him the beer; he gives you a stake. Dialogue: “Yes. Here you go”.
  6. Prepare to fight Count Draynor (level 34) and enter Draynor Manor.
  7. Enter Draynor Manor's basement.
  8. Open the coffin and kill Count Draynor. You need the stake and a hammer in your inventory.

  - Stake
      1. Talk to Dr. Harlow in the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock. Dialogue: “Morgan needs your help!”.
      2. Buy a beer from the bartender in the Blue Moon Inn. Dialogue: “A glass of your finest ale please”.
      3. Talk to Dr. Harlow again and give him the beer; he gives you a stake. Dialogue: “Yes. Here you go”.
  - Kill Count Draynor
      1. Prepare to fight Count Draynor (level 34) and enter Draynor Manor.
      2. Enter Draynor Manor's basement.
      3. Open the coffin and kill Count Draynor. You need the stake and a hammer in your inventory.

### S2-09 - Pirate's Treasure

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 2 quest points
- Map: Redbeard Frank, Port Sarim (3053, 3251, plane 0)
- NPC: Redbeard Frank - The Port Sarim dock, Ground floor
- Reward: 2 QP, a chest with a Gold ring, an Emerald and 450 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Coins x90 - 30 for the boat there, 30 for the way back, 30 for the rum. [item id 995]
  - Karamjan rum x1 - Buy from Zembo on Karamja for 30 gp and smuggle it in the crate with the bananas. [from: Zembo; obtained during the step; item id 431]
  - White apron x1 - It lies in Gerrant's fishing shop in Port Sarim. [from: Gerrant's Fishy Business; obtained during the step; item id 1005]
  - Spade x1 - For the treasure in Falador Park. [item id 952]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Redbeard Frank, Zembo, Luthas, Wydin, Customs officer
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Karamjan rum, White apron, Spade, Banana, Pirate message
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm in search of treasure.” → “Ok, I will bring you some rum” → “That customs officer is annoying isn't she?” → “Could you offer me employment on your plantation?” → “Can I journey on this ship?” → “Search away, I have nothing to hide.” → “Well, can I get a job here?” → “Ok thanks, I'll go and get it.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Pirate's Treasure
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Redbeard Frank in Port Sarim. Dialogue: “I'm in search of treasure” → “Yes”.
  2. Talk to one of the Seamen on the docks in Port Sarim to sail to Musa Point (30 gp). Dialogue: “Yes please”.
  3. Talk to Zembo in the Karamja Wines, Spirits and Beers bar and buy one Karamjan rum (30 gp). Dialogue: “Yes please”.
  4. Pick 10 bananas nearby, then talk to Luthas about working for him. Dialogue: “Could you offer me employment on your plantation?” → “Will you pay me for another crate full?”.
  5. Put the Karamjan rum into the crate.
  6. Fill the rest of the crate with bananas (right-click Fill), then talk to Luthas.
  7. Talk to Luthas and tell him you finished filling the crate.
  8. Head back to Port Sarim and pay the Customs officer to sail there (30 gp). Dialogue: “Thank you, I'll be on my way” → “Can I journey on this ship?” → “Search away, I have nothing to hide” → “Ok” → “Search away. I have nothing to hide” → “Okay”.
  9. Grab the white apron from the Port Sarim Fishing Shop (it hangs on the back wall).
  10. Wear the apron and ask Wydin for a job in the Port Sarim food shop, then search the crate in the back room for the rum. Dialogue: “Well, can I get a job here?”.
  11. Bring the Karamjan rum to Redbeard Frank in Port Sarim; he gives you a key.
  12. Climb up the stairs in the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock.
  13. Open the chest on the first floor with the key and take the Pirate message. Dialogue: “Ok thanks, I'll go and get it”.
  14. Read the Pirate message.
  15. Dig in the middle of the cross in Falador Park, and kill the Gardener (level 4) who appears. Once killed, dig again.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Talk to Redbeard Frank
      1. Talk to Redbeard Frank in Port Sarim. Dialogue: “I'm in search of treasure” → “Yes”.
  - Smuggle rum
      1. Talk to one of the Seamen on the docks in Port Sarim to sail to Musa Point (30 gp). Dialogue: “Yes please”.
      2. Talk to Zembo in the Karamja Wines, Spirits and Beers bar and buy one Karamjan rum (30 gp). Dialogue: “Yes please”.
      3. Pick 10 bananas nearby, then talk to Luthas about working for him. Dialogue: “Could you offer me employment on your plantation?” → “Will you pay me for another crate full?”.
      4. Put the Karamjan rum into the crate.
      5. Fill the rest of the crate with bananas (right-click Fill), then talk to Luthas.
      6. Talk to Luthas and tell him you finished filling the crate.
  - Back to Port Sarim
      1. Head back to Port Sarim and pay the Customs officer to sail there (30 gp). Dialogue: “Thank you, I'll be on my way” → “Can I journey on this ship?” → “Search away, I have nothing to hide” → “Ok” → “Search away. I have nothing to hide” → “Okay”.
      2. Grab the white apron from the Port Sarim Fishing Shop (it hangs on the back wall).
      3. Wear the apron and ask Wydin for a job in the Port Sarim food shop, then search the crate in the back room for the rum. Dialogue: “Well, can I get a job here?”.
      4. Bring the Karamjan rum to Redbeard Frank in Port Sarim; he gives you a key.
  - Find the treasure
      1. Climb up the stairs in the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock.
      2. Open the chest on the first floor with the key and take the Pirate message. Dialogue: “Ok thanks, I'll go and get it”.
      3. Read the Pirate message.
      4. Dig in the middle of the cross in Falador Park, and kill the Gardener (level 4) who appears. Once killed, dig again.

### S2-10 - Prince Ali Rescue

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 3 quest points
- Comes after: S1-04, S2-01
- Map: Chancellor Hassan, the Al Kharid palace (3298, 3163, plane 0)
- NPC: Chancellor Hassan - The Al Kharid palace, Ground floor
- Bronze bar: Smelt 1 copper and 1 tin ore from step S1-12 in any furnace (Furnace), for example in Lumbridge or Al Kharid.
- Rope: Buy from Ned for 18 gp or give him 4 balls of wool.
- Reward: 3 QP, 700 Coins, free permanent passage through the Al Kharid gate.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Ball of wool x3 - The ones saved from S1-04! [item id 1759]
  - Yellow dye x1 - To dye the wig. From step S2-01. [item id 1765]
  - Soft clay x1 - Use the clay on a bucket of water. [item id 1761]
  - Bronze bar x1 - Smelt the copper and tin ore kept from step S1-12 in any furnace. [item id 2349]
  - Pink skirt x1 - From step S2-01. [item id 1013]
  - Ashes x1 - The one kept from step S1-08: ashes stay on the spot of a burnt-out fire. [item id 592]
  - Redberries x1 - From step S2-01. [item id 1951]
  - Pot of flour x1 - The spare one from step S1-03. [item id 1933]
  - Bucket of water x1 - Fill it at any fountain or well. [item id 1929]
  - Beer x3 - For the guard Joe. Buy at any pub for 2 gp each. [item id 1917]
  - Rope x1 - Buy from Ned for 18 gp. [from: Ned; item id 954]
  - Coins x40 - Passing through the Al Kharid gate costs 10 gp each time. [item id 995]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Chancellor Hassan, Osman, Ned, Aggie, Lady Keli, Leela, Joe, Prince Ali
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Ball of wool, Yellow dye, Soft clay, Bronze bar, Pink skirt, Ashes, Redberries, Pot of flour, Bucket of water, Beer, Rope
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Is there anything I can help you with?” → “What is the first thing I must do?” → “What is the second thing you need?” → “Okay, I better find some things.” → “Could you make other things apart from rope?” → “How about some sort of wig?” → “I have them here. Please make me a wig.” → “Can you make skin paste?” → “Yes please. Mix me some skin paste.” → “Heard of you? You're famous in Gielinor!” → “What is your latest plan then?” → “How do you know someone won't try to free him?” → “Could I see the key please?” → “Could I touch the key for a moment please?” → “I have some beer here, fancy one?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Prince Ali Rescue
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Hassan in the Al Kharid Palace. Dialogue: “Is there anything I can help you with?” → “Yes”.
  2. Talk to Osman north of the Al Kharid Palace.
  3. Have Ned in Draynor Village make you a wig from 3 balls of wool. He also sells rope: 15 coins via dialogue, 18 coins from his shop, or 4 balls of wool. Dialogue: “Could you make other things apart from rope?” → “How about some sort of wig?” → “I have them here. Please make me a wig”.
  4. Dye the wig with yellow dye. If you need a yellow dye, Aggie in Draynor Village makes one from two onions and 5 coins.
  5. Talk to Aggie in Draynor Village to get some paste. She needs ashes, redberries, a pot of flour and a bucket of water. Dialogue: “Can you make skin paste?” → “Yes please. Mix me some skin paste”.
  6. Talk to Lady Keli in the jail east of Draynor Village and take a print of the key: use soft clay on the key. If you have already made the print, open the quest journal to re-sync. Dialogue: “Heard of you? You're famous in Gielinor!” → “What's your latest plan then?” → “How do you know someone won't try to free him?” → “Could I see the key please?” → “Could I touch the key for a moment please?”.
  7. Use the key print on any furnace with a bronze bar in your inventory to make a bronze key (the nearest furnace is in Lumbridge).
  8. Talk to Leela east of Draynor Village.
  9. Bring everything to the jail and give Joe three beers. Dialogue: “I have some beer here. Fancy one?”.
  10. Use the rope on Lady Keli to tie her up.
  11. Use the key on the prison door. If Lady Keli respawned you will need to tie her up again.
  12. Talk to Prince Ali and free him (wear the wig, skirt and paste).
  13. Return to Hassan in the Al Kharid Palace to complete the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Hassan in the Al Kharid Palace. Dialogue: “Is there anything I can help you with?” → “Yes”.
      2. Talk to Osman north of the Al Kharid Palace.
  - Blonde wig
      1. Have Ned in Draynor Village make you a wig from 3 balls of wool. He also sells rope: 15 coins via dialogue, 18 coins from his shop, or 4 balls of wool. Dialogue: “Could you make other things apart from rope?” → “How about some sort of wig?” → “I have them here. Please make me a wig”.
      2. Dye the wig with yellow dye. If you need a yellow dye, Aggie in Draynor Village makes one from two onions and 5 coins.
  - Skin paste
      1. Talk to Aggie in Draynor Village to get some paste. She needs ashes, redberries, a pot of flour and a bucket of water. Dialogue: “Can you make skin paste?” → “Yes please. Mix me some skin paste”.
  - Key print
      1. Talk to Lady Keli in the jail east of Draynor Village and take a print of the key: use soft clay on the key. If you have already made the print, open the quest journal to re-sync. Dialogue: “Heard of you? You're famous in Gielinor!” → “What's your latest plan then?” → “How do you know someone won't try to free him?” → “Could I see the key please?” → “Could I touch the key for a moment please?”.
  - The key
      1. Use the key print on any furnace with a bronze bar in your inventory to make a bronze key (the nearest furnace is in Lumbridge).
  - Bring everything to Leela
      1. Talk to Leela east of Draynor Village.
  - Free Ali
      1. Bring everything to the jail and give Joe three beers. Dialogue: “I have some beer here. Fancy one?”.
      2. Use the rope on Lady Keli to tie her up.
      3. Use the key on the prison door. If Lady Keli respawned you will need to tie her up again.
      4. Talk to Prince Ali and free him (wear the wig, skirt and paste).
  - Return to Al Kharid
      1. Return to Hassan in the Al Kharid Palace to complete the quest.

### S2-11 - Ernest the Chicken

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 4 quest points
- Map: Veronica by Draynor Manor (3110, 3328, plane 0)
- NPC: Veronica - By the gate of Draynor Manor, Ground floor
- The puzzle: The levers A, B, C, D, E, F in the basement are solved strictly by the Quest Helper arrows.
- Important: On the way out of the manor pick up the Bronze med helm from the floor: you need it for the Black Knights' Fortress quest!
- Reward: 4 QP, 300 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green and the helm is in the bank.
- Picture: The diagram of the basement levers A, B, C, D, E, F
- Keep for S3-02: Bronze med helm x1 (bank) - Black Knights' Fortress needs a Bronze med helm to get in.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Veronica, Professor Oddenstein
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Pressure gauge, Rubber tube, Oil can
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Aha, sounds like a quest. I'll help.” → “I'm looking for a guy called Ernest.” → “Change him back this instant!”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Ernest the Chicken and holding 1 x Bronze med helm
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Veronica outside of Draynor Manor. Dialogue: “Aha, sounds like a quest. I'll help”.
  2. Enter Draynor Manor's ground floor.
  3. Go upstairs.
  4. Pick up the fish food in the south room.
  5. Go back downstairs.
  6. Pick up the poison from the north-west room.
  7. Use the poison on the fish food.
  8. Pick up the spade in the east room and exit the manor through the door in the same room.
  9. Search the compost heap west of the manor for a key.
  10. Use the poisoned fish food on the fountain south-west of the manor.
  11. Search the fountain for the pressure gauge.
  12. Go back into the manor's ground floor.
  13. Unlock the small room north of the staircase with the key and pick up the rubber tube.
  14. Search the bookcase in the west room to enter the secret room.
  15. Climb down the ladder into the basement.
  16. Pull down lever A.
  17. Pull down lever B.
  18. Pull down lever D.
  19. Pull up lever B.
  20. Pull up lever A.
  21. Pull down lever F.
  22. Pull down lever E.
  23. Pull down lever C.
  24. Pull up lever E.
  25. Pick up the oil can in the west room.
  26. Talk to Professor Oddenstein on the top floor of the manor. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a guy called Ernest” → “Change him back this instant!”.
  27. Give Professor Oddenstein the pressure gauge, rubber tube and oil can to finish the quest.

### S2-12 - Goblin Diplomacy

- stage 2 (Varrock, teleport magic and key quests); type quest; 5 quest points
- Comes after: S2-10
- Map: General Bentnoze, Goblin Village (2958, 3512, plane 0)
- NPC: General Bentnoze - Goblin Village north of Falador, next to General Wartface, Ground floor
- Reward: 5 QP, 200 Crafting XP, a gold bar.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Goblin mail x3 - Drops from goblins in Goblin Village. [from: Goblin Village; obtained during the step; item id 288]
  - Orange dye x1 - Buy it, or have Aggie in Draynor Village make it from a Red dye (3 redberries + 5 gp) and a Yellow dye (2 onions + 5 gp). Get this before you leave Draynor. [item id 1769]
  - Blue dye x1 - From step S2-01 or from the witch Aggie: 2 Woad leaves (the gardener Wyson in Falador Park, 20 gp) + 5 gp. [from: Aggie; item id 1767]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: General Bentnoze, General Wartface
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Goblin mail, Orange dye, Blue dye, Orange goblin mail, Blue goblin mail
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Do you want me to pick an armour colour for you?” → “Start the quest?” → “What about a different colour?” → “I have some blue armour here” → “I have some brown armour here”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Goblin Diplomacy
- Walkthrough:
  1. You need three goblin mails, which you can find around Goblin Village. The first is up the ladder in a crate in the south of the village.
  2. The second goblin mail is in a crate in the western hut.
  3. The third goblin mail is in a crate north of the generals' hut.
  4. Use the blue dye on one of the goblin mails.
  5. Use the orange dye (a mix of red and yellow) on another goblin mail.
  6. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals in Goblin Village and show them the orange armour. Dialogue: “Do you want me to pick an armour colour for you?” → “What about a different colour?” → “Yes” → “So how is life for the goblins?” → “Yes, Wartface looks fat” → “I have some orange armour here”.
  7. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals again and show them the blue armour. Dialogue: “So how is life for the goblins?” → “I have some blue armour here”.
  8. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals once more and show them the plain (brown) armour to finish the quest. Dialogue: “So how is life for the goblins?” → “Yes, Wartface looks fat” → “I have some brown armour here”.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):

      2. The second goblin mail is in a crate in the western hut.
      3. The third goblin mail is in a crate north of the generals' hut.
      4. Use the blue dye on one of the goblin mails.
      5. Use the orange dye (a mix of red and yellow) on another goblin mail.
  - Show the armour
      1. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals in Goblin Village and show them the orange armour. Dialogue: “Do you want me to pick an armour colour for you?” → “What about a different colour?” → “Yes” → “So how is life for the goblins?” → “Yes, Wartface looks fat” → “I have some orange armour here”.
      2. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals again and show them the blue armour. Dialogue: “So how is life for the goblins?” → “I have some blue armour here”.
      3. Talk to one of the Goblin Generals once more and show them the plain (brown) armour to finish the quest. Dialogue: “So how is life for the goblins?” → “Yes, Wartface looks fat” → “I have some brown armour here”.

## Stage 3 - Access to the mines and gear preparation

### S3-01 - Doric's Quest

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Doric's house (2952, 3450, plane 0)
- NPC: Doric - A hut north of Falador, Ground floor
- Reward: 1 QP, 1,300 Mining XP, 180 Coins, access to Doric's anvils.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Clay x6 - From step S2-01. [item id 434]
  - Copper ore x4 - From step S2-01. [item id 436]
  - Iron ore x2 - From step S2-01. [item id 440]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Doric
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Clay, Copper ore, Iron ore
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I wanted to use your anvils.” → “Yes, I will get you the materials.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Doric's Quest
- Walkthrough:
  1. Bring Doric north of Falador 6 clay, 4 copper ore and 2 iron ore; afterwards he lets you use his anvils. You can mine them all in the Dwarven Mine, or buy them from the Grand Exchange. Dialogue: “I wanted to use your anvils” → “Yes” → “Yes, I will get you the materials”.
