### S3-02 - Black Knights' Fortress

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type quest; 3 quest points; needs 12 quest points
- Comes after: S2-11
- Map: Sir Amik Varze, White Knights' Castle (2960, 3339, plane 2)
- Other places on the map:
  - Cabbage — the farm field south of Falador (3043, 3294, plane 0) [items: Cabbage] - The cabbage from Draynor Manor will not do for the quest.
- NPC: Sir Amik Varze - White Knights' Castle in Falador, 2nd floor
- Disguise: Wear the iron chainbody and the bronze helm: the guard will let you into the fortress. The Black Knights (level 33) inside attack: bring 5 trout.
- Reward: 3 QP, 2 500 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Iron chainbody x1 - Buy from Horvik in Varrock or Wayne in Falador. [from: Wayne; item id 1101]
  - Bronze med helm x1 - From step S2-11 or buy in the helmet shop in Barbarian Village. [from: Peksa; item id 1139]
  - Cabbage x1 - Pick it on the cabbage field south of Falador: the cabbage from Draynor Manor does not work! [obtained during the step; item id 1965]
- Items recommended:
  - Trout x5 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 24 gp. [heals 7; item id 333]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Sir Amik Varze
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Iron chainbody, Bronze med helm, Cabbage
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I seek a quest!” → “I laugh in the face of danger!” → “Ok, I'll do my best.” → “I don't care. I'm going in anyway.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Black Knights' Fortress
- Walkthrough:
  1. Speak to Sir Amik Varze on the 2nd floor of Falador Castle. Dialogue: “I seek a quest!” → “I laugh in the face of danger!” → “Yes”.
  2. Enter the Black Knights' Fortress wearing an iron chainbody and a bronze med helm. Level 33 Black Knights attack you inside.
  3. Push the wall to enter a secret room.
  4. Climb up the ladder.
  5. Climb up the next ladder.
  6. Climb down the ladder south of you.
  7. Climb up the ladder east of you.
  8. Climb down the next ladder east of you.
  9. Climb down the ladder west of you.
  10. Listen at the grill.
  11. Climb back up the ladder from the listening room.
  12. Climb up the ladder in the chapel room east of you.
  13. Climb down the ladder west of you.
  14. Climb up the ladder west of you.
  15. Climb down the ladder north of you.
  16. Climb down the next ladder.
  17. Go into the east room and climb the ladder there. At the door you get a dialogue: select option 2. Dialogue: “I don't care. I'm going in anyway”.
  18. Push the wall to the south-east to enter the storage room.
  19. USE the cabbage on the hole. Be careful not to eat it! The cabbage must come from the field south of Falador.
  20. Return to Sir Amik Varze in Falador Castle to complete the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Sir Amik Varze
      1. Speak to Sir Amik Varze on the 2nd floor of Falador Castle. Dialogue: “I seek a quest!” → “I laugh in the face of danger!” → “Yes”.
  - Infiltrate the fortress
      1. Enter the Black Knights' Fortress wearing an iron chainbody and a bronze med helm. Level 33 Black Knights attack you inside.
      2. Push the wall to enter a secret room.
      3. Climb up the ladder.
      4. Climb up the next ladder.
      5. Climb down the ladder south of you.
      6. Climb up the ladder east of you.
      7. Climb down the next ladder east of you.
      8. Climb down the ladder west of you.
      9. Listen at the grill.
  - Ruin the potion
      1. Climb back up the ladder from the listening room.
      2. Climb up the ladder in the chapel room east of you.
      3. Climb down the ladder west of you.
      4. Climb up the ladder west of you.
      5. Climb down the ladder north of you.
      6. Climb down the next ladder.
      7. Go into the east room and climb the ladder there. At the door you get a dialogue: select option 2. Dialogue: “I don't care. I'm going in anyway”.
      8. Push the wall to the south-east to enter the storage room.
      9. USE the cabbage on the hole. Be careful not to eat it! The cabbage must come from the field south of Falador.
  - Return to Sir Amik Varze
      1. Return to Sir Amik Varze in Falador Castle to complete the quest.

### S3-03 - Demon Slayer

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type quest; 3 quest points
- Map: The gypsy Aris, Varrock Square (3203, 3424, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Lumbridge cow field — bones from the cows (3257, 3272, plane 0) [NPC Cow] [items: Bones]
- NPC: Aris - A tent on Varrock Square, Ground floor
- Combat: The demon Delrith (level 27) at the stone circle south of Varrock. Finish him only with the sword Silverlight, speaking the incantation from the gypsy.
- Reward: 3 QP, the sword Silverlight.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Bones x25 - For Traiborn. They drop from cows, chickens and goblins. Bank everything else first: 25 bones leave almost no room in the bag. You can hand them in in parts. [item id 526]
  - Bucket of water x1 - Pour it into the drain by the Varrock Palace kitchen. [item id 1929]
  - Coins x1 - To the gypsy Aris. [item id 995]
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Varrock Teleport [varrock-teleport] - needs magic 25
      also: Law rune - 1 of Law rune
      also: Air rune - 3 of Air rune (not needed with Staff of air, Air battlestaff, Mystic air staff)
      also: Fire rune - 1 of Fire rune (not needed with Staff of fire, Fire battlestaff, Mystic fire staff, Lava battlestaff, Mystic lava staff)
      goes to: Varrock Teleport — the square by the fountain (3213, 3424, plane 0)
      instead: Magic tab, Varrock Teleport (1 **Law rune**, 3 **Air rune**, 1 **Fire rune**): you are on the square by the fountain. The gypsy Aris is in the tent on the same square.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Aris, Sir Prysin, Captain Rovin, Wizard Traiborn, Delrith
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Bones, Bucket of water, Silverlight
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yes.” → “Okay, where is he? I'll kill him for you!” → “So how did Wally kill Delrith?” → “Where can I find Silverlight?” → “Aris said I should come and talk to you.” → “I need to find Silverlight.” → “He's back and unfortunately I've got to deal with him.” → “And why is this a problem?” → “Yes I know, but this is important.” → “There's a demon who wants to invade this city.” → “Yes, very.” → “It's not them who are going to fight the demon, it's me.” → “Sir Prysin said you would give me the key.” → “Why did he give you one of the keys then?” → “Talk about Demon Slayer.” → “Well, have you got any keys knocking around?” → “I'll get the bones for you.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Demon Slayer
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Aris in her tent in Varrock Square (give her 1 gp). Dialogue: “The Demon Slayer Quest” → “Yes” → “Ok, here you go” → “Okay, where is he? I'll kill him for you!” → “So how did Wally kill Delrith?”.
  2. Talk to Sir Prysin in the south-west corner of Varrock Castle. Dialogue: “Aris said I should come and talk to you” → “I need to find Silverlight” → “He's back and unfortunately I've got to deal with him” → “So give me the keys!” → “Can you give me your key?”.
  3. Talk to Captain Rovin upstairs in the north-west of Varrock Castle. Dialogue: “Yes I know, but this is important” → “There's a demon who wants to invade this city” → “Yes, very” → “It's not them who are going to fight the demon, it's me” → “Sir Prysin said you would give me the key” → “Why did he give you one of the keys then?”.
  4. Use the bucket of water on the drain outside the palace kitchen.
  5. Go down into the Varrock Sewers via the manhole south-east of Varrock Castle.
  6. Pick up the rusty key north of the sewer entrance.
  7. Bring Wizard Traiborn in the Wizards' Tower 25 bones. You don't need to bring them all at once. Dialogue: “Talk about Demon Slayer” → “I need to get a key given to you by Sir Prysin” → “Well, have you got any keys knocking around?” → “I'll get the bones for you”.
  8. Return to Sir Prysin in the south-west of Varrock Castle and give him the three keys to receive Silverlight.
  9. Kill Delrith (level 27) with Silverlight by the dark wizards south of Varrock; once he is down, say the incantation Aris gave you (pick the words in order in the dialogue).

### S3-04 - Below Ice Mountain

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type quest; 1 quest point; needs 16 quest points
- Requirements:
  - mining 10 (needed during the quest; you can start without it) (can be boosted)
- Map: Willow tree by Ice Mountain (3002, 3436, plane 0)
- NPC: Willow - The path south of Ice Mountain, Ground floor
- Warning: The last fight: the Ancient Guardian keeps attacking while you mine its pillars. Bring a pickaxe and 10-15 Trout or Salmon, check Mining 10, and eat before each pillar. The details are in the lines below.
- Requirements: Mining 10 (a boost counts) and any pickaxe for the final fight; 16 quest points to start the quest.
- Combat: The final room: the Ancient Guardian (level 25) has 40 hit points, hits up to 7 and attacks about every 3 seconds. It does not stop while you mine. Eat before you start the next pillar; do not wait until you are almost dead.
- Recommended method: Mine the four Structural pillars in the corners of the room with the Mine option. Each one breaks and hurts the Guardian. You need Mining 10 and any pickaxe, and these are objects of the room, not ore rocks.
- Bring: A pickaxe (the Bronze pickaxe from S1-12 is enough) and 10-15 Trout or Salmon, or better food. You will not pass a bank before this fight.
- Can't mine it?: Check three things: Mining level 10 or more (a boost counts); a pickaxe in the bag or in your hands (the game says "You need a pickaxe you have the Mining level to use to mine the pillar"); and that you clicked a Structural pillar, not a rock, a wall or a Broken pillar.
- Fallback: safespot: If the Guardian hurts you faster than you can mine, lure it to the east side of the chamber and retreat into the entrance passage: it gets stuck on the corner. Then attack it with Ranged or Magic. Direct melee works at any level but is the riskiest choice for the account this route produces.
- Reward: 1 QP, 2,000 Coins, access to the Camdozaal ruins, the Flex emote.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Cooked meat x1 - Cook the raw cow meat. [item id 2142]
  - Bread x1 - Buy at the bakery or bake it yourself. [item id 2309]
  - Knife x1 - Lies in the house north-west of Lumbridge Castle or in the General Store. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 946]
  - Beer x1 - For Burntof. 2 gp at any pub. [item id 1917]
  - Bronze pickaxe x1 - Any pickaxe works: the Bronze pickaxe from step S1-12 is enough. It is for the Structural pillars in the last room, not for ore. [item id 1265]
- Items recommended:
  - Trout x10 - 10-15 Trout or Salmon (about 30 gp each on the Grand Exchange), or better food you already own. For the Ancient Guardian, which keeps attacking while you mine. [any of: Salmon, Tuna, Lobster, Swordfish, Monkfish, Shark, Cooked meat, Cake, Pie; heals 7; item id 333]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Willow, Checkal, Atlas, Marley, Cook, Burntof, Ancient Guardian
  - Objects highlighted: Structural pillar
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Cooked meat, Bread, Knife, Beer, Steak sandwich
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yes.” → “I was wondering if you'd be able to make me a Steak sandwich?” → “What ales are you serving?” → “One Asgarnian Ale, please.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Below Ice Mountain
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Willow outside Falador, south of the Ice Mountain dwarves. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Attempt to recruit Checkal in Barbarian Village.
  3. Speak to Atlas in the Barbarian Village Inn to learn how to Flex. Pick up a cooked meat if you need one. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  4. Talk to Checkal and flex your muscles to prove your worth.
  5. Speak to Marley in the Edgeville ruins. Pick up the cooked meat from the Barbarian longhall if you don't have it already.
  6. Ask the Cook at the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock for a steak sandwich. Dialogue: “I was wondering if you'd be able to make me a Steak sandwich?”.
  7. Collect cooked meat, bread and a knife to make the steak sandwich.
  8. Use the knife on the bread to make a steak sandwich. Be careful not to eat it!
  9. Return to Marley and give him the steak sandwich. Be careful not to eat it!
  10. Talk to Burntof in the Rising Sun Inn in Falador.
  11. Buy an Asgarnian Ale for Burntof. Dialogue: “What ales are you serving?” → “One Asgarnian Ale, please”.
  12. Give Burntof the Asgarnian Ale.
  13. Beat Burntof in a match of Rock-Paper-Scissors. Your choices of Rock, Paper and Scissors do not matter. Dialogue: “Rock”.
  14. Talk to Willow at the dungeon entrance on the west side of Ice Mountain. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  15. Before you go in: take a pickaxe (any) and 10-15 Trout or Salmon, and check Mining is 10 or more. There is no bank inside.
  16. Enter the dungeon again to finish the quest.
  17. In the final room do not wait for the Ancient Guardian to leave you alone: it keeps attacking while you mine.
  18. Mine a Structural pillar in one of the four corners (the Mine option). The pillar breaks and hurts the Guardian.
  19. Eat when you need to (not later than when your health is within two hits of 7), then move straight to the next Structural pillar.
  20. Repeat until the Ancient Guardian is defeated. If it hurts you faster than you mine, use the safespot: lure it to the east side and retreat into the entrance passage.
  21. Watch the cutscene at the entrance to finish the quest. Dialogue: “Yes”.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Willow outside Falador, south of the Ice Mountain dwarves. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  - Checkal
      1. Attempt to recruit Checkal in Barbarian Village.
      2. Speak to Atlas in the Barbarian Village Inn to learn how to Flex. Pick up a cooked meat if you need one. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      3. Talk to Checkal and flex your muscles to prove your worth.
  - Marley
      1. Speak to Marley in the Edgeville ruins. Pick up the cooked meat from the Barbarian longhall if you don't have it already.
      2. Ask the Cook at the Blue Moon Inn in Varrock for a steak sandwich. Dialogue: “I was wondering if you'd be able to make me a Steak sandwich?”.
      3. Collect cooked meat, bread and a knife to make the steak sandwich.
      4. Use the knife on the bread to make a steak sandwich. Be careful not to eat it!
      5. Return to Marley and give him the steak sandwich. Be careful not to eat it!
  - Burntof
      1. Talk to Burntof in the Rising Sun Inn in Falador.
      2. Buy an Asgarnian Ale for Burntof. Dialogue: “What ales are you serving?” → “One Asgarnian Ale, please”.
      3. Give Burntof the Asgarnian Ale.
      4. Beat Burntof in a match of Rock-Paper-Scissors. Your choices of Rock, Paper and Scissors do not matter. Dialogue: “Rock”.
  - The dig
      1. Talk to Willow at the dungeon entrance on the west side of Ice Mountain. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      2. Before you go in: take a pickaxe (any) and 10-15 Trout or Salmon, and check Mining is 10 or more. There is no bank inside.
      3. Enter the dungeon again to finish the quest.
      4. In the final room do not wait for the Ancient Guardian to leave you alone: it keeps attacking while you mine.
      5. Mine a Structural pillar in one of the four corners (the Mine option). The pillar breaks and hurts the Guardian.
      6. Eat when you need to (not later than when your health is within two hits of 7), then move straight to the next Structural pillar.
      7. Repeat until the Ancient Guardian is defeated. If it hurts you faster than you mine, use the safespot: lure it to the east side and retreat into the entrance passage.
      8. Watch the cutscene at the entrance to finish the quest. Dialogue: “Yes”.

### S3-05 - Shield of Arrav

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type quest; optional; 1 quest point
- Map: Reldo, the Varrock Palace library (3210, 3494, plane 0)
- NPC: Reldo - The Varrock Palace library, Ground floor
- Feature: A cooperative quest (Phoenix Gang against the Black Arm Gang). Join the chat channel (Grouping, Clan chat, osrs soa) and find a partner. Or skip it: you have enough points for the dragon without it.
- Reward: 1 QP, 600 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green (or knowingly skipped).
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Varrock Teleport [varrock-teleport] - needs magic 25
      also: Law rune - 1 of Law rune
      also: Air rune - 3 of Air rune (not needed with Staff of air, Air battlestaff, Mystic air staff)
      also: Fire rune - 1 of Fire rune (not needed with Staff of fire, Fire battlestaff, Mystic fire staff, Lava battlestaff, Mystic lava staff)
      goes to: Varrock Teleport — the square by the fountain (3213, 3424, plane 0)
      instead: Magic tab, Varrock Teleport (1 **Law rune**, 3 **Air rune**, 1 **Fire rune**): you are on the square by the fountain. The Palace library is north of the square.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Reldo
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm in search of a quest.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Shield of Arrav
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Reldo in the Varrock Palace library. Dialogue: “I'm in search of a quest.” → “Yes.”.
  2. Search the marked bookcase in the library for the book, then read it (left-click the book in your inventory).
  3. Talk to Reldo again. Dialogue: “Okay, I've read that book about the Shield of Arrav.”.
  4. You and a partner join different gangs: one the Phoenix Gang, the other the Black Arm Gang. You cannot change later. The chat channel “OSRS SOA” finds a partner.
  5. Talk to Baraek in Varrock Square: ask about the Phoenix Gang, not furs. Dialogue: “Can you tell me where I can find the Phoenix Gang?” → “Okay. Have 20 gold coins.”.
  6. Climb down the ladder into the Phoenix Gang hideout in south-east Varrock and talk to Straven. Dialogue: “I know who you are!” → “I'd like to offer you my services.”.
  7. Kill Jonny the Beard (level 2) in the Blue Moon Inn and pick up the Intel report.
  8. Give the Intel report to Straven in the hideout: he gives you the Weapon store key.
  9. Search the chest in the hideout for half of the Shield of Arrav.
  10. Talk to Charlie the Tramp at the south entrance of Varrock. Dialogue: “Is there anything down this alleyway?” → “I'm looking for the Black Arm Gang.” → “Do you think they would let me join?”.
  11. Talk to Katrine down the alley to the west and join her gang. Dialogue: “I want to become a member of your gang.” → “Well, you can give me a try can't you?” → “Ok, no problem.”.
  12. Get the Weapon store key from your Phoenix Gang partner, climb the ladder into the weapon storeroom in south-east Varrock, kill the Weaponsmaster (level 23) and pick up both Phoenix crossbows.
  13. Bring both crossbows to Katrine, go up the stairs in the gang base and search the cupboard for half of the Shield of Arrav.
  14. Pass the Weapon store key to your partner, then take your half of the shield to Curator Haig Halen in the Varrock Museum: he gives you half of a certificate.
  15. Swap certificate halves with a player of the other gang (a partner or a second account; alone, skip the quest: it is optional) and use the two halves together.
  16. Give the Certificate to King Roald in Varrock Palace to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Start the quest
      1. Talk to Reldo in the Varrock Palace library. Dialogue: “I'm in search of a quest.” → “Yes.”.
      2. Search the marked bookcase in the library for the book, then read it (left-click the book in your inventory).
      3. Talk to Reldo again. Dialogue: “Okay, I've read that book about the Shield of Arrav.”.
  - Choose a gang
      1. You and a partner join different gangs: one the Phoenix Gang, the other the Black Arm Gang. You cannot change later. The chat channel “OSRS SOA” finds a partner.
  - Phoenix Gang
      1. Talk to Baraek in Varrock Square: ask about the Phoenix Gang, not furs. Dialogue: “Can you tell me where I can find the Phoenix Gang?” → “Okay. Have 20 gold coins.”.
      2. Climb down the ladder into the Phoenix Gang hideout in south-east Varrock and talk to Straven. Dialogue: “I know who you are!” → “I'd like to offer you my services.”.
      3. Kill Jonny the Beard (level 2) in the Blue Moon Inn and pick up the Intel report.
      4. Give the Intel report to Straven in the hideout: he gives you the Weapon store key.
      5. Search the chest in the hideout for half of the Shield of Arrav.
  - Black Arm Gang
      1. Talk to Charlie the Tramp at the south entrance of Varrock. Dialogue: “Is there anything down this alleyway?” → “I'm looking for the Black Arm Gang.” → “Do you think they would let me join?”.
      2. Talk to Katrine down the alley to the west and join her gang. Dialogue: “I want to become a member of your gang.” → “Well, you can give me a try can't you?” → “Ok, no problem.”.
      3. Get the Weapon store key from your Phoenix Gang partner, climb the ladder into the weapon storeroom in south-east Varrock, kill the Weaponsmaster (level 23) and pick up both Phoenix crossbows.
      4. Bring both crossbows to Katrine, go up the stairs in the gang base and search the cupboard for half of the Shield of Arrav.
  - Finish together
      1. Pass the Weapon store key to your partner, then take your half of the shield to Curator Haig Halen in the Varrock Museum: he gives you half of a certificate.
      2. Swap certificate halves with a player of the other gang (a partner or a second account; alone, skip the quest: it is optional) and use the two halves together.
      3. Give the Certificate to King Roald in Varrock Palace to finish the quest.

### S3-06 - Money for the Adamant scimitar: iron ore

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type prep
- Better after (not required): S3-05
- Where: The mine south-east of Varrock (3 iron veins) or the Al Kharid mine (free passage after Prince Ali). The bank is Varrock East or Al Kharid.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The mine south-east of Varrock (iron) (3286, 3365, plane 0)
- How: Mine 25 to 30 iron ore (Mining 15: mine copper and tin up to 15 first) and sell it on the Grand Exchange. Stop as soon as you have 2,500 gp in coins: ore in the bag is not cash until it is sold. This step pays only for the Adamant scimitar, not for a whole armour set. Mining levels along the way: you will need level 60 in S6-03.
- How much you need: Only the Adamant scimitar (S3-07): about 1,400 gp at the prices of 26 Sep 2026, so 2,500 gp is the target with room to spare. The stage 3 quests already give about 5,300 coins (Black Knights' Fortress 2,500, Below Ice Mountain 2,000, Shield of Arrav 600, Doric 180), so you may have it before you mine anything. Armour is skipped on purpose: see S3-08. The 10,000 gp for Wormbrain (S5-05) is paid later, from the stage 4 loot (S4-03).
- Price: Iron ore is about 73 gp on 26 Sep 2026: 25 ores are about 1,800 gp and 30 about 2,200 gp. If you do not need the ore for quests, sell it at once.
- Other ways: Cowhides to leather with Ellis in Al Kharid (about 260 gp per leather), and from Attack 40 big bones (Big bones, about 310 gp) from the Hill Giants of step S4-03.
- New account: A new free-to-play account cannot sell iron ore (or leather) on the Grand Exchange until it has 20 hours of play, 10 quest points and total level 100. The stage 3 quests usually pay the 2,500 gp anyway; if they have not, mine only what the quests need and wait for the big bones of S4-03, which can be sold.
- Money goal: 2,500 coins in the bag and the bank by the end of the step
- Done when: 2,500+ coins in the bag or the bank (unsold ore does not count).
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Objects highlighted: Iron rocks
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 2500 x Coins
- sells: ["Iron ore","Leather"]

### S3-07 - Buy the Adamant scimitar on the Grand Exchange

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type gear
- Map: The Grand Exchange (3164, 3487, plane 0)
- Requirement: The Adamant scimitar can be wielded only with 30 Attack: buy it now, wield it after step S3-08.
- Budget: About 1,400 gp at the prices of 26 Sep 2026.
- Armour: No armour is bought here. Adamant armour needs 30 Defence, and Defence is left alone on purpose (see S3-08): Dragon Slayer I gives 18,650 Defence XP, which is level 33 from level 1. Wear what you already have.
- Amulet: A second amulet is not needed: by the damage formulas the Amulet of strength from S2-01 hits faster than the Amulet of power in every combat step (strength +10 against +6). Take the Amulet of power (+6 to accuracy, strength and defence) only if you have no Amulet of strength: almost the same speed and a little defence.
- Done when: The Adamant scimitar is bought and is in the bank.
- Items required:
  - Adamant scimitar x1 - Needs 30 Attack. About 1,400 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 1331]
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Varrock Teleport [varrock-teleport] - needs magic 25
      also: Law rune - 1 of Law rune
      also: Air rune - 3 of Air rune (not needed with Staff of air, Air battlestaff, Mystic air staff)
      also: Fire rune - 1 of Fire rune (not needed with Staff of fire, Fire battlestaff, Mystic fire staff, Lava battlestaff, Mystic lava staff)
      goes to: Varrock Teleport — the square by the fountain (3213, 3424, plane 0)
      instead: Magic tab, Varrock Teleport (1 **Law rune**, 3 **Air rune**, 1 **Fire rune**): you are on the square by the fountain. The Grand Exchange is north-west of the square.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 1 x Adamant scimitar

### S3-08 - Attack to 30 and Strength to 20

- stage 3 (Access to the mines and gear preparation); type skill
- Members alternative: 👑 With membership: do not fight mobs by hand: complete Waterfall Quest in 15 minutes and get 30 Attack and 30 Strength at once!
- Where: The Stronghold of Security, floor 1 (the Vault of War): the Minotaurs, level 12 (and 27 further in). The entrance is in the Barbarian Village mine, the same place as S1-09. There is no bank on the floor: bank in Edgeville or Varrock West first.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The Stronghold of Security, floor 1: the minotaurs (3081, 3420, plane 0)
- How: Train Attack to 30 first on the Accurate style, then Strength to 20 on Aggressive (up to 25 if you like). Defence is not trained: Dragon Slayer I (S5-08) gives 18,650 Defence XP, which is level 33 from level 1, so grinding it now would waste the reward.
- Training spot: A Minotaur has 10 hit points and gives about 40 XP per kill; the pace line counts the kills left from your real Attack and Strength XP (earlier quest rewards already count). They hit for up to 2 (up to 3 at level 27) and are not aggressive, so you choose when to fight.
- Defence: Leave it at 1. The Anti-dragon shield from S5-02 stops Elvarg's fire at any Defence level.
- Done when: Attack 30 and Strength 20. Defence is left alone.
- Pace: attack to level 30 (13,363 XP): minotaur|minotaurs, 40 XP each
- Fights in melee: Minotaur
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Minotaur
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: attack 30, strength 20

## Stage 4 - Preparing for Dragon Slayer I

### S4-01 - The Corsair Curse

- stage 4 (Preparing for Dragon Slayer I); type quest; 2 quest points
- Map: Captain Tock north of Port Sarim (3030, 3273, plane 0)
- NPC: Captain Tock - The crossroads north of Port Sarim, Ground floor
- Combat: Ithoi the Navigator (level 35) hits up to 6: hit him in melee or with magic, and eat a salmon before your health falls to 12 (two of his hits), not after.
- Reward: 2 QP, access to the Yusuf bank in Corsair Cove and to the Corsair Cove Dungeon.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Fights in melee: Ithoi the Navigator
- Items recommended:
  - Salmon x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 30 gp. [heals 9; item id 329]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Captain Tock, Ithoi the Navigator, Gnocci the Cook, Arsen the Thief, Cabin Boy Colin, Chief Tess
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “What kind of help do you need?” → “Sure, I'll try to help with your curse.” → “Okay, I'm ready to go to Corsair Cove.” → “I hear you've been cursed.” → “Arsen says he gave you a sacred ogre relic.” → “I've come to return what Arsen stole.” → “I've ruled out all the Corsairs' theories...” → “So what do I do now?” → “I hear it happened straight after dinner.” → “I hear Ithoi cooked the meal you ate that night.” → “What is the mission Francois is doing?” → “I hear the Captain's thinking of firing Ithoi.” → “I hear you cooked the meal they ate before getting sick.” → “Maybe because the Captain's thinking of firing you.” → “I know you've faked the curse.” → “I bet I can prove you're well enough to get up.” → “I saw you running around! You're not sick!” → “I've seen Ithoi running around. He's not sick at all.” → “I'll be back.” → “I've killed Ithoi for poisoning your crew.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: The Corsair Curse
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Captain Tock north of Port Sarim. Dialogue: “What kind of help do you need?” → “Sure, I'll try to help with your curse”.
  2. Talk to Captain Tock west of Rimmington to sail to Corsair Cove. Dialogue: “Okay, I'm ready go to Corsair Cove”.
  3. Talk to Ithoi in the south-western hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
  4. Talk to Gnocci in the north-west hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
  5. Talk to Arsen in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
  6. Talk to Colin in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
  7. Pick up the tinderbox next to Gnocci.
  8. Take the spade in the south of Corsair Cove.
  9. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “Arsen says he gave you a sacred ogre relic” → “About that sacred ogre relic”.
  10. Talk to Chief Tess in the Corsair Cove Dungeon and return the relic. Dialogue: “I've come to return what Arsen stole”.
  11. Dig next to the tree south-west of the cove to find the doll. Dialogue: “Search for the possessed doll and face the consequences”.
  12. Go look through Ithoi's telescope.
  13. Talk to Gnocci again in the north-west hut.
  14. Talk to Arsen again in the central hut.
  15. Talk to Colin again in the central hut.
  16. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “I've ruled out all the Corsairs' theories” → “So what do I do now?”.
  17. Talk to Gnocci in the north-west hut. Dialogue: “I hear it happened straight after dinner”.
  18. Talk to Arsen in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear Ithoi cooked the meal you ate that night”.
  19. Talk to Ithoi and confront him: he is not actually sick. Dialogue: “I bet I can prove you're well enough to get up” → “I know you've faked the curse” → “I hear you cooked the meal they ate before getting sick” → “Maybe because the Captain's thinking of firing you”.
  20. Use your tinderbox on the driftwood under Ithoi's hut.
  21. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “I've seen Ithoi running around. He's not sick at all”.
  22. Kill Ithoi (level 35). Fight him with melee or magic, and eat when your health is below 15.
  23. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship to finish the quest. Dialogue: “I've killed Ithoi for poisoning your crew”.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Sail to Corsair Cove
      1. Talk to Captain Tock north of Port Sarim. Dialogue: “What kind of help do you need?” → “Sure, I'll try to help with your curse”.
      2. Talk to Captain Tock west of Rimmington to sail to Corsair Cove. Dialogue: “Okay, I'm ready go to Corsair Cove”.
  - Investigate the curse
      1. Talk to Ithoi in the south-western hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
      2. Talk to Gnocci in the north-west hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
      3. Talk to Arsen in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
      4. Talk to Colin in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear you've been cursed”.
      5. Pick up the tinderbox next to Gnocci.
      6. Take the spade in the south of Corsair Cove.
      7. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “Arsen says he gave you a sacred ogre relic” → “About that sacred ogre relic”.
      8. Talk to Chief Tess in the Corsair Cove Dungeon and return the relic. Dialogue: “I've come to return what Arsen stole”.
      9. Dig next to the tree south-west of the cove to find the doll. Dialogue: “Search for the possessed doll and face the consequences”.
      10. Go look through Ithoi's telescope.
      11. Talk to Gnocci again in the north-west hut.
      12. Talk to Arsen again in the central hut.
      13. Talk to Colin again in the central hut.
  - Expose the traitor
      1. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “I've ruled out all the Corsairs' theories” → “So what do I do now?”.
      2. Talk to Gnocci in the north-west hut. Dialogue: “I hear it happened straight after dinner”.
      3. Talk to Arsen in the central hut. Dialogue: “I hear Ithoi cooked the meal you ate that night”.
      4. Talk to Ithoi and confront him: he is not actually sick. Dialogue: “I bet I can prove you're well enough to get up” → “I know you've faked the curse” → “I hear you cooked the meal they ate before getting sick” → “Maybe because the Captain's thinking of firing you”.
      5. Use your tinderbox on the driftwood under Ithoi's hut.
      6. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship. Dialogue: “I've seen Ithoi running around. He's not sick at all”.
  - Deal with Ithoi
      1. Kill Ithoi (level 35). Fight him with melee or magic, and eat when your health is below 15.
      2. Talk to Captain Tock on the ship to finish the quest. Dialogue: “I've killed Ithoi for poisoning your crew”.

### S4-02 - The Ides of Milk

- stage 4 (Preparing for Dragon Slayer I); type quest; 1 quest point
- Map: Cassius by the Lumbridge pond (3168, 3275, plane 0)
- NPC: Cassius - By the pond in Lumbridge, Ground floor
- Warning: Brutus's specials (Snort and Growl) are avoided by moving two tiles to the side, not by eating. Bring 5 Salmon for the plain hits.
- Combat: The final fight is against Brutus. In the quest his plain hits cannot kill you (they leave you at 1 health), but his specials can: they ignore protection prayers and kill you if they land. His plain hit is up to 3. After 1 to 6 hits he uses a special that is not stopped by food or protection prayers: Snort hits the row in front of him (or an L shape if you stand diagonal), Growl is a charge at you. Step two tiles to either side, or one tile back from Snort, as soon as he winds up. If it goes wrong, run south-west to the gate.
- Reward: 1 QP, access to the cow boss Brutus, a 1,000 XP lamp for any combat skill or Prayer (put it into Prayer) and the Cowbell amulet from Gillie Groats.
- Done when: The quest is green and the lamp from Gillie Groats has been used on Prayer.
- Fights in melee: Brutus
- Items recommended:
  - Salmon x5 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 30 gp. [heals 9; item id 329]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Take the Cowbell amulet from Gillie Groats: she gives it and the XP lamp after the fight. [checked from the bag and bank: 1 x Cowbell amulet]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Cassius, Gillie Groats, Brutus
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yes.” → “Can you share what makes your cows so productive?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: The Ides of Milk
- Walkthrough:
  1. Speak to Cassius by the pond north-west of Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Speak to Gillie Groats at the cow field east of Lumbridge. Dialogue: “Can you share what makes your cows so productive?”.
  3. Speak to Seth Groats at his farmhouse east of the River Lum.
  4. Search the shelves in Seth's farmhouse for 'The groats principles'.
  5. Return to Cassius with the book.
  6. Drink the milk sample near Cassius.
  7. Talk to Cassius after drinking the milk sample; he gives you a second sample.
  8. Speak to Duke Horacio on the first floor of Lumbridge Castle.
  9. Speak to Gillie Groats at the cow field.
  10. Drink the milk sample near Gillie.
  11. Talk to Gillie Groats after drinking the milk sample.
  12. Open the bull pen gate in the north-east corner of the cow field to fight the bull (level 30). Dialogue: “Yes”.
  13. Speak to Gillie Groats after defeating the bull.
  14. Return to Cassius to complete the quest. You can then speak to Gillie Groats for a cow bell amulet and a magic lamp (1,000 XP combat/Prayer).
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Speak to Cassius by the pond north-west of Lumbridge to start the quest. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  - Investigation
      1. Speak to Gillie Groats at the cow field east of Lumbridge. Dialogue: “Can you share what makes your cows so productive?”.
      2. Speak to Seth Groats at his farmhouse east of the River Lum.
      3. Search the shelves in Seth's farmhouse for 'The groats principles'.
  - Milk tasting
      1. Return to Cassius with the book.
      2. Drink the milk sample near Cassius.
      3. Talk to Cassius after drinking the milk sample; he gives you a second sample.
  - The Duke's opinion
      1. Speak to Duke Horacio on the first floor of Lumbridge Castle.
      2. Speak to Gillie Groats at the cow field.
      3. Drink the milk sample near Gillie.
      4. Talk to Gillie Groats after drinking the milk sample.
  - The bull fight
      1. Open the bull pen gate in the north-east corner of the cow field to fight the bull (level 30). Dialogue: “Yes”.
      2. Speak to Gillie Groats after defeating the bull.
  - The end
      1. Return to Cassius to complete the quest. You can then speak to Gillie Groats for a cow bell amulet and a magic lamp (1,000 XP combat/Prayer).

### S4-03 - Attack to 40 and bank 50 Big bones

- stage 4 (Preparing for Dragon Slayer I); type skill
- Members alternative: 👑 With membership: do not fight mobs by hand: complete Waterfall Quest in 15 minutes and get 30 Attack and 30 Strength at once!
- Where: The Hill Giants in the Edgeville Dungeon: the trapdoor is in Edgeville, a few steps south-west of the bank, and the giants are in the south-east of the dungeon. If you have a Brass key, the locked door by the giants saves the walk past the skeletons. The Edgeville bank is a minute away: bank the bones there.
- Map: The trapdoor to the Edgeville Dungeon (3097, 3468, plane 0)
- Goals: Two in one block: Attack 40 with the Adamant scimitar, and at least 50 Big bones banked. A Hill Giant has 35 hit points, about 140 XP a kill, and always drops Big bones.
- Money: Bank the Big bones, do not sell them in the dungeon: 50 of them are about 14,000-16,000 gp at the Grand Exchange (check the price when you sell). This pays for Wormbrain (10,000 gp in S5-05), the ship (2,000 gp in S5-06) and the materials in S4-05; the gap, if the price has moved, is shown by the preparation list. Bones in the bank are not coins until sold.
- Weapon: Train with the Adamant scimitar. The Rune scimitar can be wielded only from Attack 40: once you reach it, the gear advice may offer it, it is not part of this step.
- Armour: No Defence training, so wear what you already have. The Anti-dragon shield (S5-02) stops Elvarg's fire whatever your Defence is, and the 18,650 Defence XP from Dragon Slayer I lifts Defence by itself. Hill Giants hit up to 4 and are aggressive: eat before your health falls to 8 (two hits), and earlier when two of them are on you.
- Done when: Attack 40 and 50+ Big bones in the bank or the bag. Strength and Defence are left alone.
- Pace: attack to level 40 (37,224 XP): giant|giants, 140 XP each
- Fights in melee: Hill Giant
- Items required:
  - Adamant scimitar x1 - From step S3-07. Wear it until Attack 40: the Rune scimitar needs 40 Attack. [item id 1331]
- Items recommended:
  - Salmon x20 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 30 gp (20 of them about 600). [heals 9; item id 329]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Hill Giant
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Big bones
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: attack 40 and holding 50 x Big bones

### S4-04 - Food for the dragon: buy 20 Lobster

- stage 4 (Preparing for Dragon Slayer I); type gear
- Where: The Grand Exchange: buy the cooked Lobster in one go. No trip to Karamja.
- Map: The Grand Exchange (3164, 3487, plane 0)
- How: Buy 20 cooked Lobster on the Grand Exchange: about 105 gp each, about 2,100 gp for 20 at the prices of 26 Sep 2026 (keep up to 4,000 gp in mind if the price moves). This replaces the old Karamja cage-fishing loop: lobsters need Fishing 40 and Cooking 40 and a boat each way, hours of work that add nothing to the fight. Any lobsters that drop on the way count towards the 20.
- Why not catch them: Lobsters need Fishing 40 and Cooking 40 and a boat to Musa Point, hours of work that add nothing to the fight. Buying them is far faster.
- Done when: 20+ cooked Lobster in the bag or the bank.
- Items required:
  - Lobster x20 - Buy cooked Lobster on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp each (20 of them about 2,100). Heals 12. [heals 12; item id 379]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Lobster
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 20 x Lobster

### S4-05 - Buy all the materials for the dragon on the Grand Exchange

- stage 4 (Preparing for Dragon Slayer I); type gear
- Map: The Grand Exchange (3164, 3487, plane 0)
- How: Do not forge nails by hand (it needs Smithing 34)! Buy everything on the Grand Exchange: it comes to about 5,000 gp together with the 2,000 coins for the ship.
- Done when: All the items are stacked in the bank.
- Items required:
  - Steel nails x90 - 30 nails for each plank, about 30 gp each. [obtained during the step; item id 1539]
  - Plank x3 - Only ordinary planks, about 140 gp. [obtained during the step; item id 960]
  - Hammer x1 - Buy in the Lumbridge General Store for 1 gp. [from: Lumbridge General Store; obtained during the step; item id 2347]
  - Unfired bowl x1 - Buy on the Grand Exchange or shape it from soft clay on the pottery wheel (Crafting 8) in Barbarian Village. [obtained during the step; item id 1791]
  - Wizard's mind bomb x1 - Buy in the Rising Sun pub in Falador for 3 gp. [from: Rising Sun Inn; obtained during the step; item id 1907]
  - Silk x1 - Buy from the silk trader in Al Kharid for 3 gp. [from: Silk trader; obtained during the step; item id 950]
  - Lobster pot x1 - Buy from Gerrant in Port Sarim for 20 gp (more expensive on the Grand Exchange, about 500 gp). Needed for the Oracle (S5-04). [from: Gerrant; obtained during the step; item id 301]
  - Coins x2000 - For buying the ship Lady Lumbridge. [obtained during the step; item id 995]
- Quick options (offered when you qualify):
  - Varrock Teleport [varrock-teleport] - needs magic 25
      also: Law rune - 1 of Law rune
      also: Air rune - 3 of Air rune (not needed with Staff of air, Air battlestaff, Mystic air staff)
      also: Fire rune - 1 of Fire rune (not needed with Staff of fire, Fire battlestaff, Mystic fire staff, Lava battlestaff, Mystic lava staff)
      goes to: Varrock Teleport — the square by the fountain (3213, 3424, plane 0)
      instead: Magic tab, Varrock Teleport (1 **Law rune**, 3 **Air rune**, 1 **Fire rune**): you are on the square by the fountain. The Grand Exchange is north-west of the square.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 90 x Steel nails; 3 x Plank; 1 x Hammer; 1 x Unfired bowl; 1 x Wizard's mind bomb; 1 x Silk; 1 x Lobster pot; 2000 x Coins

## Stage 5 - Dragon Slayer I: the F2P climax

### S5-01 - Start at Champions' Guild

- stage 5 (Dragon Slayer I: the F2P climax); type quest; needs 32 quest points
- Map: Guildmaster, Champions' Guild (3191, 3358, plane 0)
- NPC: Guildmaster - Champions' Guild south of the Varrock gate (teleport with the Chronicle book), Ground floor
- How: Talk to the guildmaster, then go to Oziach in Edgeville. Come back to the Guildmaster and ask absolutely every question in turn (about the dragon, the ship and the fire protection): he gives you the key to the maze (Maze key).
- Requirement: 32 QP (by this point you have 44 QP).
- Recommended: Combat level 45+, Prayer 37 for Protect from Magic: the dragon hits with magic too.
- Done when: You have the Maze key.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Guildmaster, Oziach
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Maze key
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Can I have a quest?” → “Can you sell me a rune platebody?” → “The Guildmaster of the Champions' Guild told me.” → “I thought you were going to give me a quest.” → “A dragon, that sounds like fun.” → “I talked to Oziach...” → “How can I find the route to Crandor?” → “Where is Melzar's map piece?” → “Where is Thalzar's map piece?” → “Where is Lozar's map piece?” → “Where can I find the right ship?” → “How can I protect myself from the dragon's breath?” → “Okay, I'll get going!”
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 1 x Maze key
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to the Guildmaster in the Champions' Guild, south of Varrock. Needs 32 quest points. Dialogue: “Can I have a quest?”.
  2. Talk to Oziach in his hut in north-western Edgeville. Dialogue: “Can you sell me a rune platebody?” → “The Guildmaster of the Champions' Guild told me.” → “I thought you were going to give me a quest.” → “A dragon, that sounds like fun.”.
  3. Return to the Guildmaster and ask every question in turn: the ship, the shield, Melzar's, Thalzar's and Lozar's map pieces. Asking about Melzar's piece gives you the Maze key.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to the Guildmaster in the Champions' Guild, south of Varrock. Needs 32 quest points. Dialogue: “Can I have a quest?”.
      2. Talk to Oziach in his hut in north-western Edgeville. Dialogue: “Can you sell me a rune platebody?” → “The Guildmaster of the Champions' Guild told me.” → “I thought you were going to give me a quest.” → “A dragon, that sounds like fun.”.
  - The Guildmaster's questions
      1. Return to the Guildmaster and ask every question in turn: the ship, the shield, Melzar's, Thalzar's and Lozar's map pieces. Asking about Melzar's piece gives you the Maze key.

- Enemies by room: Zombie rat (red key), ghost (orange), skeleton (yellow), zombie (blue), Melzar the Mad, level 43 (purple), Lesser demon, level 82 (green key).

### S5-08 - The battle with the dragon Elvarg

- stage 5 (Dragon Slayer I: the F2P climax); type quest
- Comes after: S5-07, S5-02
- Map: Port Sarim docks: the Lady Lumbridge (3047, 3205, plane 0)
- Equipment: The Anti-dragon shield is worn: without it the dragon's fire hits up to 70, with the shield up to 10. Optional: a Rune sword on the Stab style (Elvarg's weakest defence is against stab), an Amulet of strength (from S2-01; if you do not have one, an Amulet of power).
- Inventory: 20+ lobsters (Lobster, +12 health each), 1 Energy potion, a teleport to Varrock. The more lobsters, the lower the risk: you can fill the rest of the space with them too.
- Route: Sail to Crandor, climb to the top of the mountain, go down into the volcano and first open the secret door in the south wall: it is a shortcut from the Karamja Volcano cave in case the dragon kills you.
- Combat tactics: Elvarg is level 83 with 80 health. Use the Stab style if you have the Rune sword, otherwise your best scimitar. With the shield Elvarg hits up to 10: eat a lobster before your health falls to 20 (two hits), not after. Do not take the shield off for a second!
- Done when: The dragon is beheaded and Elvarg's head is in your inventory.
- Other opponents (food advice): Elvarg
- Items required:
  - Anti-dragon shield x1 - Worn. [item id 1540]
  - Lobster x20+ - From step S4-04, or buy more on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp each. The more lobsters, the lower the risk. [heals 12; item id 379]
- Items recommended:
  - Rune sword x1 - Optional: a stab attack on the Stab style (about 12,000 gp, Attack 40). Your Adamant scimitar works without it. [item id 1289]
  - Energy potion(4) x1 - Optional: restores run energy on the climb to the volcano. From S2-01 or on the Grand Exchange for about 170 gp. [item id 3008]
  - Strength potion(4) x1 - Optional: one dose before you enter Elvarg's lair. Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 100 gp. [item id 113]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Captain Ned, Elvarg
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Anti-dragon shield, Rune sword, Lobster, Energy potion(4), Strength potion(4)
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yep, sounds good.”
  - Marked done automatically when: all of: 1 x Elvarg's head
- Walkthrough:
  1. Do not board without the Anti-dragon shield, food and a strong weapon.
  2. Board Lady Lumbridge in Port Sarim and talk to Ned on the deck.
  3. Go to the centre of the island and down the hole.
  4. Go all the way south and through the wall: it unlocks the shortcut from the Karamja volcano.
  5. Enter Elvarg's area and kill her. Her head ends up in your bag.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Sail to Crandor
      1. Do not board without the Anti-dragon shield, food and a strong weapon.
      2. Board Lady Lumbridge in Port Sarim and talk to Ned on the deck.
  - Crandor and the volcano
      1. Go to the centre of the island and down the hole.
      2. Go all the way south and through the wall: it unlocks the shortcut from the Karamja volcano.
      3. Enter Elvarg's area and kill her. Her head ends up in your bag.

        - Strength potion(4) - Optional boost: a strength potion before the fight.

### S5-09 - Triumph at Oziach

- stage 5 (Dragon Slayer I: the F2P climax); type quest; 2 quest points
- Comes after: S5-08

- Reward: 2 QP, 18,650 XP each in Defence and Strength, the right to wear the Rune platebody and the Green d'hide body. All 46 points of F2P quests are collected!

## Stage 6 - Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)

### S6-01 - Ranged to 50 on the Ogress Warriors and Shamans

- stage 6 (Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)); type skill; optional; the way to a Bond (skipped on a members account)
- Comes after: S4-01
- Where: The Corsair Cove Dungeon cave (open after the quest The Corsair Curse).
- Map: Corsair Cove Dungeon (2012, 9004, plane 0)
- Optional branch: This whole stage is only the way to a Bond for an account without membership. Nothing in it is required for the Members route. If you already have membership, switch the app to Members mode: stage 6 disappears and Waterfall Quest comes next. Do the parts that pay best for you, in any order.
- Why: One of the best places in F2P for money while you train Ranged: ogresses (level 82) drop a Rune med helm, a Rune battleaxe, nature runes and gems.
- How: Ogresses hit hard (up to 8) and you train only Ranged here: shoot them from a safespot with a bow and keep melee out of it. Use the best bow your Ranged allows with the best arrows you can afford, and 20 lobsters or swordfish for the walk back.
- Magic instead: To train Magic on the same ogresses, use step S6-04: it is a separate goal.
- Done when: Ranged 50.
- Other opponents (food advice): Ogress warrior, Ogress shaman
- Items recommended:
  - Swordfish x20 - Catch with a harpoon from Fishing 50 at Musa Point (step S6-02) or buy on the Grand Exchange. [heals 14; item id 373]
  - Lobster x20 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp (20 of them about 2,100). Or catch them yourself from Fishing 40 with a Lobster pot (20 gp in the shop Gerrant's Fishy Business in Port Sarim) at the Musa Point dock on Karamja (boat from Port Sarim, 30 gp) and cook them from Cooking 40 on the range in Al Kharid. [heals 12; item id 379]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Ogress Warrior, Ogress Shaman
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: ranged 50

### S6-02 - Fishing to 50 and Cooking to 50

- stage 6 (Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)); type skill; optional; the way to a Bond (skipped on a members account)
- Where: 40-50: lobsters at Musa Point (Karamja). From 50: swordfish (Swordfish) with a harpoon in the same place. Cook on the range in Al Kharid.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: Lobsters and swordfish at Musa Point (2923, 3180, plane 0)
- Important: Swordfish can only be caught from Fishing 50 and cooked from Cooking 45. Before that, lobsters (from 40) or tuna with a harpoon (from 35).
- Optional: One of the ways to earn towards a Bond. Skip it if another way pays you better.
- Done when: Fishing 50, Cooking 50.
- Items required:
  - Lobster pot x1 - For lobsters up to 50. [item id 301]
  - Harpoon x1 - For tuna and swordfish. From Gerrant in Port Sarim it is 5 gp (about 450 gp on the Grand Exchange). [item id 311]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Fishing spot
  - Objects highlighted: Range
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Lobster pot, Harpoon
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: fishing 50, cooking 50

### S6-03 - Mining to 60 (Mining Guild) and Woodcutting to 60 (yews)

- stage 6 (Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)); type skill; optional; the way to a Bond (skipped on a members account)
- Map: Iron ore — the mine south-east of Varrock (3286, 3365, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Iron ore — the mine south-east of Varrock (Mining up to 60) (3286, 3365, plane 0)
  - Iron ore — Al Kharid mine (Mining up to 60) (3298, 3293, plane 0)
  - Willow trees — Draynor Village (Woodcutting 30-60) (3103, 3265, plane 0)
- Mining: Up to 60: iron in the mine south-east of Varrock or in Al Kharid (from 30, coal in Barbarian Village). From 60 the Mining Guild under Falador opens: lots of coal and mithril.
- Woodcutting: Up to 60: willows (Willow, from 30) by Draynor Village. Yews (Yew) can only be chopped from 60: by Edgeville, Varrock Palace and south of Falador. Yew logs are about 105 gp: a good passive income.
- Optional: One of the ways to earn towards a Bond. Skip it if another way pays you better.
- Done when: Mining 60, Woodcutting 60.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Objects highlighted: Iron rocks, Coal rocks, Mithril rocks, Willow tree, Yew tree
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: mining 60, woodcutting 60

### S6-04 - Magic to 55 (High Level Alchemy)

- stage 6 (Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)); type skill; optional; the way to a Bond (skipped on a members account)
- How: Up to 43: combat spells on monsters: Fire Strike, from 35 Fire Bolt. From 43, Superheat Item on iron ore: a bar always comes out and Smithing levels along the way. At 55 High Level Alchemy opens: turn unneeded rune loot from the ogresses into coins.
- Optional: One of the ways to earn towards a Bond. Skip it if another way pays you better.
- Done when: Magic 55.
- Items required:
  - Nature rune xAs many as casts - About 140 gp each. The High Level Alchemy value of the item minus the item's price minus this rune must stay above zero: check the live prices in the app before you cast. [item id 561]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: magic 55

### S6-05 - Buy an Old School Bond on the Grand Exchange

- stage 6 (Optional: earn a Bond in F2P (skip it if you already have membership)); type prep; the way to a Bond (skipped on a members account)
- Map: The Grand Exchange (3164, 3487, plane 0)
- How: An Old School Bond costs about 11.5 million gp (price of 26 Sep 2026, the live price is in the app) and gives 14 days of membership. For F2P that is a lot: save up on the ogresses, yews and swordfish of this stage, or pay for membership with money: progress does not depend on the method. When membership is active, switch the app to Members mode: that opens the Members route.
- Done when: Membership is activated.
- Items required:
  - Old school bond x1 - Buy on the Grand Exchange (about 11.5 million gp on 26 Sep 2026, check the live price). It gives 14 days of membership: use it on yourself. [obtained during the step; item id 13190]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Old school bond
  - No automatic completion: you mark this step yourself.

## Part 2 - Members route (16 steps)

## Stage 7 - Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding

### S7-01 - Waterfall Quest

- stage 7 (Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding); type quest; members only; 1 quest point
- Map: Almera's house by Baxtorian Falls (2520, 3495, plane 0)
- NPC: Almera - A house north-east of Baxtorian Falls, Ground floor
- Notes: There are no combat requirements, but fire giants (level 86) walk the Waterfall dungeon and Moss Guardians (level 84, up to 14) the tomb. The tomb refuses weapons, armour, runes, arrows and logs: go in with Glarial's pebble and food only, then return to the bank for the runes, the rope, the amulet, the urn and food, and run past the giants.
- Reward: 1 QP, 13,750 Attack XP and 13,750 Strength XP: your melee level jumps instantly from 1 to 30+!
- Done when: The quest is green, Attack and Strength 30+.
- Items required:
  - Rope x1 - Buy from Ned in Draynor Village for 16 gp or on the Grand Exchange for about 65 gp. [from: Ned; item id 954]
  - Air rune x6 - For the six pillars in the last room of the Waterfall dungeon, not for the tomb: the tomb refuses runes. Take them from the bank after the tomb. [loadout: dungeon; item id 556]
  - Earth rune x6 - For the six pillars in the last room of the Waterfall dungeon, not for the tomb: the tomb refuses runes. Take them from the bank after the tomb. [loadout: dungeon; item id 557]
  - Water rune x6 - For the six pillars in the last room of the Waterfall dungeon, not for the tomb: the tomb refuses runes. Take them from the bank after the tomb. [loadout: dungeon; item id 555]
  - Glarial's pebble x1 - The gnome Golrie in the Tree Gnome Village dungeon gives it to you during the quest. [from: Golrie; obtained during the step; loadout: tomb; item id 294]
- Items recommended:
  - Lobster x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange (about 105 gp each). Take a few into the tomb too, the rest for the dungeon. [heals 12; loadout: dungeon; item id 379]
- Inventories by phase:
  - Almera to the Tree Gnome dungeon - Nothing is hostile here. Talk to Almera, then Hudon on the island, read the Book on Baxtorian, enter the Tree Gnome Village dungeon and talk to Golrie.
      take Rope x1 - Ned in Draynor Village, or the Grand Exchange
  - Glarial's Tomb, keep 2 slots free - Jewellery is allowed. Moss Guardians hit up to 14: start a fight with a skeleton or zombie yourself so they cannot attack you first.
      take Glarial's pebble x1 - from Golrie
      take Trout x5 - or any food
      leave behind: weapons, armour (capes too), runes, arrows, logs
  - Waterfall dungeon, keep 5 slots free - Weapons and armour are allowed here, but you do not need them: run past the fire giants. Do not click "Take treasure" before using the urn on the chalice.
      take Rope x1
      take Air rune x6
      take Water rune x6
      take Earth rune x6
      take Glarial's amulet x1 - from the tomb
      take Glarial's urn x1 - from the tomb
      take Trout x10 - or any food
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Almera, Hudon
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Rope, Air rune, Earth rune, Water rune, Glarial's pebble, Glarial's amulet, Glarial's urn
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “How can I help?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Waterfall Quest
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Almera on top of Baxtorian Falls, south of Barbarian Outpost. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Board the log raft west of Almera.
  3. Talk to Hudon on the island.
  4. Use a rope on the rock to the south.
  5. Use a rope on the dead tree.
  6. Get in the barrel.
  7. Go up the stairs in the house south of Almera's house (Hadley's house).
  8. Search the south-east bookcase.
  9. Read the book on Baxtorian.
  10. Go to the centre of the Tree Gnome Village and go down the ladder at the entrance.
  11. Search the off-coloured crate in the east room.
  12. Go through the gate in the west room.
  13. Talk to Golrie; he gives you Glarial's pebble.
  14. Bank everything besides the pebble and some food (no weapons or armour!). Then use Glarial's pebble on Glarial's tombstone east of Baxtorian Falls.
  15. Search the chest in the western room for Glarial's amulet.
  16. Search Glarial's tomb in the south room for Glarial's urn.
  17. Leave Glarial's tomb and get 6 air, water and earth runes, a rope, Glarial's amulet, Glarial's urn and some food.
  18. Board the log raft west of Almera again.
  19. Use a rope on the rock to the south.
  20. Use a rope on the dead tree.
  21. Equip Glarial's amulet.
  22. Enter the falls with Glarial's amulet equipped.
  23. Search the crate in the east room for a key.
  24. Enter the west room with the key.
  25. Use 1 earth, water and air rune on each of the 6 pillars in the room. Afterwards, use Glarial's amulet on the statue of Glarial.
  26. DO NOT LEFT-CLICK THE CHALICE! Use Glarial's urn on the chalice to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Almera on top of Baxtorian Falls, south of Barbarian Outpost. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  - Investigation
      1. Board the log raft west of Almera.

      3. Use a rope on the rock to the south.
      4. Use a rope on the dead tree.
      5. Get in the barrel.
      6. Go up the stairs in the house south of Almera's house (Hadley's house).
      7. Search the south-east bookcase.
      8. Read the book on Baxtorian.
  - Glarial's pebble
      1. Go to the centre of the Tree Gnome Village and go down the ladder at the entrance.
      2. Search the off-coloured crate in the east room.
      3. Go through the gate in the west room.
      4. Talk to Golrie; he gives you Glarial's pebble.
  - Glarial's tomb
      1. Bank everything besides the pebble and some food (no weapons or armour!). Then use Glarial's pebble on Glarial's tombstone east of Baxtorian Falls.
      2. Search the chest in the western room for Glarial's amulet.
      3. Search Glarial's tomb in the south room for Glarial's urn.
  - Lay Glarial to rest
      1. Leave Glarial's tomb and get 6 air, water and earth runes, a rope, Glarial's amulet, Glarial's urn and some food.
      2. Board the log raft west of Almera again.
      3. Use a rope on the rock to the south.
      4. Use a rope on the dead tree.
      5. Equip Glarial's amulet.
      6. Enter the falls with Glarial's amulet equipped.

      8. Enter the west room with the key.
      9. Use 1 earth, water and air rune on each of the 6 pillars in the room. Afterwards, use Glarial's amulet on the statue of Glarial.
      10. DO NOT LEFT-CLICK THE CHALICE! Use Glarial's urn on the chalice to finish the quest.

### S7-02 - Fight Arena

- stage 7 (Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding); type quest; members only; 2 quest points
- Map: Lady Servil by the Fight Arena (2566, 3199, plane 0)
- NPC: Lady Servil - West of Ardougne Monastery, by the north-east corner of the gnome maze, Ground floor
- Bring: A bow with arrows or runes for magic and 5 gp for Khali brew.
- Safespot: All the opponents, the scorpion (44), the ogre (63) and the Bouncer (137), can be hit from cover with a bow or magic: they all fight only up close. General Khazard (142) is optional: you do not need to kill him for the quest.
- Reward: 2 QP, 12 175 XP Attack, 2 175 XP Thieving, 1 000 Coins.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Khazard helmet x1 - Take it from the chest in the arena armoury during the quest. [obtained during the step; item id 74]
  - Khazard armour x1 - Take it from the chest in the arena armoury during the quest. [obtained during the step; item id 75]
  - Coins x5 - For the Khali brew for the guard. [item id 995]
- Items recommended:
  - Lobster x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp (20 of them about 2,100). Or catch them yourself from Fishing 40 with a Lobster pot (20 gp in the shop Gerrant's Fishy Business in Port Sarim) at the Musa Point dock on Karamja (boat from Port Sarim, 30 gp) and cook them from Cooking 40 on the range in Al Kharid. [heals 12; item id 379]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Lady Servil, Khazard Barman
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Khazard helmet, Khazard armour, Khali brew
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Can I help you?” → “I'd like a Khali brew please.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Fight Arena
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Lady Servil, west-south-west of the monastery south of Ardougne. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Search the chest to the east for some Khazard armour.
  3. Equip the Khazard armour and talk to the Khazard Guard in the south-east of the prison.
  4. Buy Khali brew from the nearby bar to the west for 5 coins. Dialogue: “I'd like a Khali Brew please”.
  5. Take the brew back to the Khazard Guard.
  6. Get another set of keys from the Khazard Guard.
  7. Get ready to fight the monsters (all safespottable), starting with the Khazard Ogre (level 63). Use the keys on Sammy's cell door to free him.
  8. Kill the ogre. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it.
  9. Talk to General Khazard.
  10. Talk to Hengrad.
  11. Kill the Khazard scorpion. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it.
  12. Kill Bouncer. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it. Warning: after Bouncer is killed, you will be unable to re-enter the arena.
  13. Exit the arena (you can ignore General Khazard). Warning: you will be unable to re-enter the arena. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  14. Go back to Lady Servil to end the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Lady Servil, west-south-west of the monastery south of Ardougne. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      2. Search the chest to the east for some Khazard armour.
      3. Equip the Khazard armour and talk to the Khazard Guard in the south-east of the prison.
      4. Buy Khali brew from the nearby bar to the west for 5 coins. Dialogue: “I'd like a Khali Brew please”.
      5. Take the brew back to the Khazard Guard.
  - Fight!
      1. Get another set of keys from the Khazard Guard.
      2. Get ready to fight the monsters (all safespottable), starting with the Khazard Ogre (level 63). Use the keys on Sammy's cell door to free him.
      3. Kill the ogre. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it.
      4. Talk to General Khazard.
      5. Talk to Hengrad.
      6. Kill the Khazard scorpion. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it.
      7. Kill Bouncer. You can lure it behind a skeleton to safespot it. Warning: after Bouncer is killed, you will be unable to re-enter the arena.
  - The end
      1. Exit the arena (you can ignore General Khazard). Warning: you will be unable to re-enter the arena. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      2. Go back to Lady Servil to end the quest.

### S7-03 - Tree Gnome Village

- stage 7 (Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding); type quest; members only; 2 quest points
- Map: King Bolren, Tree Gnome Village (2542, 3169, plane 0)
- NPC: King Bolren - The centre of the Tree Gnome Village maze, Ground floor
- Safespot: The boss Khazard warlord (level 112) can be stuck behind the trees and shot with magic.
- Reward: 2 QP, 11,450 Attack XP, the Gnome amulet, access to the Spirit trees teleport network.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Logs x6 - For the gnome ballista. Chop near the maze. [item id 1511]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: King Bolren, Commander Montai, Khazard warlord
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Logs
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Can I help at all?” → “I would be glad to help.” → “Ok, I'll gather some wood.” → “I'll try my best.” → “I will find the warlord and bring back the orbs.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Tree Gnome Village
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to King Bolren in the centre of the Tree Gnome Maze.
  2. Speak with Commander Montai, north-east of the maze entrance; he asks for 6 logs. Dialogue: “Ok, I'll gather some wood”.
  3. Speak with Commander Montai again to give him the wood.
  4. Speak with Commander Montai once more. Dialogue: “I'll try my best”.
  5. Talk to the first tracker gnome to the north-west of the battlefield for the height coordinate.
  6. Talk to the second tracker gnome inside the jail for the y coordinate.
  7. Talk to the third tracker gnome to the south-west of the jail.
  8. Fire the balista at the tower, entering the coordinates you collected.
  9. Enter the tower through the crumbled wall, climb the stairs and take the first orb from the chest.
  10. Return the orb of protection to King Bolren in the centre of the maze.
  11. Talk to the Khazard warlord, south-west of West Ardougne, ready to fight him.
  12. Defeat the Khazard warlord (level 112) and retrieve the orbs; you can lure him behind the trees and use magic.
  13. Return the orbs of protection to King Bolren in the centre of the maze to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to King Bolren in the centre of the Tree Gnome Maze.
  - Three trackers
      1. Speak with Commander Montai, north-east of the maze entrance; he asks for 6 logs. Dialogue: “Ok, I'll gather some wood”.
      2. Speak with Commander Montai again to give him the wood.
      3. Speak with Commander Montai once more. Dialogue: “I'll try my best”.
      4. Talk to the first tracker gnome to the north-west of the battlefield for the height coordinate.
      5. Talk to the second tracker gnome inside the jail for the y coordinate.
      6. Talk to the third tracker gnome to the south-west of the jail.
      7. Fire the balista at the tower, entering the coordinates you collected.
  - Retrieve the orbs
      1. Enter the tower through the crumbled wall, climb the stairs and take the first orb from the chest.
      2. Return the orb of protection to King Bolren in the centre of the maze.
      3. Talk to the Khazard warlord, south-west of West Ardougne, ready to fight him.
      4. Defeat the Khazard warlord (level 112) and retrieve the orbs; you can lure him behind the trees and use magic.
      5. Return the orbs of protection to King Bolren in the centre of the maze to finish the quest.

### S7-04 - Agility to 25 (for The Grand Tree)

- stage 7 (Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding); type skill; members only
- Where: 1-25: Gnome Stronghold Agility Course (Tree Gnome Stronghold, next to the start of The Grand Tree): no falls, no food needed. Or the Draynor Village Rooftop Course, which is also open from level 1. Both give about 10 thousand XP an hour.
- Floor: Ground floor
- Map: The Gnome Stronghold agility course (2474, 3438, plane 0)
- Why: The Grand Tree requires 25 Agility. The quest itself gives another 7,900 Agility XP: from level 25 that is about 31 at once.
- Mark of Grace: Marks of Grace appear on the roofs of the courses: pick them up, since the Graceful set is later assembled from them (S8-03).
- Done when: Agility 25.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - Arrow to: The Gnome Stronghold agility course (2474, 3438, plane 0)
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: agility 25

### S7-05 - The Grand Tree

- stage 7 (Express start of membership: a combat jump without grinding); type quest; members only; 5 quest points
- Comes after: S7-04
- Requirements:
  - agility 25 (needed during the quest; you can start without it) (can be boosted)
- Map: King Narnode Shareen, Grand Tree (2466, 3491, plane 0)
- NPC: King Narnode Shareen - Tree Gnome Stronghold, Ground floor
- Requirement: Agility 25: needed during the quest, you can start without it.
- Safespot: In the fight with the Black demon (level 172) use the safespot behind the rocks.
- Reward: 5 QP, 18,400 Attack XP, 7,900 Agility XP, 2,150 Magic XP. Opens access to the Gnome gliders.
- Done when: The quest is green, Attack 40+.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: King Narnode Shareen, Glough, Anita
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “You seem worried, what's up?” → “I'd be happy to help!” → “I think so.” → “None of the above.” → “A man came to me with the King's seal.” → “I gave the man Daconia rocks.” → “And Daconia rocks will kill the tree!” → “Glough sent me.” → “Ka.” → “Lu.” → “Min.” → “Sadly his wife is no longer with us!” → “He loves worm holes.” → “Anita.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: The Grand Tree
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to King Narnode Shareen in the Grand Tree (make sure to have two empty inventory slots to start the quest). Dialogue: “You seem worried, what's up?” → “I'd be happy to help!”.
  2. Go up to Hazelmere, on the island east of Yanille: on foot from Yanille (the fairy ring CLS opens only after Fairytale II, step S9-03).
  3. Talk to Hazelmere; he takes the bark sample and gives you a message for the king.
  4. Return to King Narnode in the Grand Tree and pass on the message. Dialogue: “I think so!” → “A man came to me with the King's seal” → “I gave the man Daconia rocks” → “And Daconia rocks will kill the tree!”.
  5. Go up to Glough in the Tree Gnome Stronghold.
  6. Talk to Glough.
  7. Talk to King Narnode again in the Grand Tree.
  8. Talk to Charlie on the top floor of the Grand Tree.
  9. Return to Charlie.
  10. Return to Glough's house and search the cupboard for Glough's journal.
  11. Talk to Glough about his journal; you get arrested.
  12. Talk to Charlie in the cell.
  13. Talk to King Narnode outside Charlie's cell.
  14. Travel with the glider (at the top of the tree) to Karamja to escape. Dialogue: “Take me to Karamja please!”.
  15. Enter the shipyard on Karamja: say Glough sent you and give the password Ka-Lu-Min. Dialogue: “Glough sent me” → “Ka” → “Lu” → “Min”.
  16. Get the lumber order from the Foreman on the southern docks. You can either kill him or talk to him (answer the security questions). Dialogue: “Sadly his wife is no longer with us!” → “He loves worm holes” → “Anita”.
  17. Talk to Anita in the house west of the Grand Tree; she gives you a key.
  18. Search the chest in Glough's house with the key and take the invasion plans (finish the dialogue).
  19. Take the invasion plans to King Narnode in the Grand Tree.
  20. Place the twigs to spell 'TUZO' in the watchtower in Glough's house. Return to King Narnode if you lost any. If your Agility is below 25, eat a summer pie.
  21. Go down the trapdoor. Be prepared for the fight against a Black demon (level 172).
  22. Kill the black demon. Stand just north of the stairs and the demon gets stuck behind the rocks; use magic, ranged or a halberd for up to 10 minutes.
  23. Talk to King Narnode deeper in the cave (finish the dialogue).
  24. Search the roots under the Grand Tree until you find the last daconia rock. If you lose the rock, it will be in the same root again.
  25. Give the daconia rock to King Narnode under the Grand Tree to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to King Narnode Shareen in the Grand Tree (make sure to have two empty inventory slots to start the quest). Dialogue: “You seem worried, what's up?” → “I'd be happy to help!”.
  - Hazelmere
      1. Go up to Hazelmere, on the island east of Yanille: on foot from Yanille (the fairy ring CLS opens only after Fairytale II, step S9-03).
      2. Talk to Hazelmere; he takes the bark sample and gives you a message for the king.
  - Investigation
      1. Return to King Narnode in the Grand Tree and pass on the message. Dialogue: “I think so!” → “A man came to me with the King's seal” → “I gave the man Daconia rocks” → “And Daconia rocks will kill the tree!”.
      2. Go up to Glough in the Tree Gnome Stronghold.
      3. Talk to Glough.
      4. Talk to King Narnode again in the Grand Tree.
      5. Talk to Charlie on the top floor of the Grand Tree.
      6. Return to Charlie.
      7. Return to Glough's house and search the cupboard for Glough's journal.
      8. Talk to Glough about his journal; you get arrested.
      9. Talk to Charlie in the cell.
      10. Talk to King Narnode outside Charlie's cell.
      11. Travel with the glider (at the top of the tree) to Karamja to escape. Dialogue: “Take me to Karamja please!”.
  - Karamja
      1. Enter the shipyard on Karamja: say Glough sent you and give the password Ka-Lu-Min. Dialogue: “Glough sent me” → “Ka” → “Lu” → “Min”.
      2. Get the lumber order from the Foreman on the southern docks. You can either kill him or talk to him (answer the security questions). Dialogue: “Sadly his wife is no longer with us!” → “He loves worm holes” → “Anita”.
  - Tuzo
      1. Talk to Anita in the house west of the Grand Tree; she gives you a key.
      2. Search the chest in Glough's house with the key and take the invasion plans (finish the dialogue).
      3. Take the invasion plans to King Narnode in the Grand Tree.
      4. Place the twigs to spell 'TUZO' in the watchtower in Glough's house. Return to King Narnode if you lost any. If your Agility is below 25, eat a summer pie.
  - The black demon
      1. Go down the trapdoor. Be prepared for the fight against a Black demon (level 172).
      2. Kill the black demon. Stand just north of the stairs and the demon gets stuck behind the rocks; use magic, ranged or a halberd for up to 10 minutes.
      3. Talk to King Narnode deeper in the cave (finish the dialogue).
      4. Search the roots under the Grand Tree until you find the last daconia rock. If you lose the rock, it will be in the same root again.
      5. Give the daconia rock to King Narnode under the Grand Tree to finish the quest.

  - Value 0 and above:
