## Stage 8 - Global transport and stamina

### S8-01 - Priest in Peril

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type quest; members only; 1 quest point
- Map: King Roald, Varrock Palace (3220, 3474, plane 0)
- NPC: King Roald - Varrock Castle, Ground floor
- Reward: 1 QP, 1,406 Prayer XP, the Wolfbane dagger, access to the region of Morytania and the town of Canifis.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Rune essence x50 - Mine from Aubury in Varrock (after Rune Mysteries) or buy on the Grand Exchange (about 12 gp). You can carry it in parts. [from: Aubury; item id 1436]
  - Bucket x1 - In the Varrock General Store for 2 gp. [from: Varrock General Store; item id 1925]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: King Roald, Drezel, Temple Guardian
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Rune essence, Bucket
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I'm looking for a quest!” → “Yes, I think I've heard of it.” → “Yes.” → “I'll get going.” → “King Roald sent me to check on Drezel.” → “What do we do?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Priest in Peril
- Walkthrough:
  1. Speak to King Roald in Varrock Castle. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest!” → “Yes”.
  2. Go to the temple east of Varrock by the river and click on the large door. Dialogue: “I'll get going” → “Roald sent me to check on Drezel” → “Sure. I'm a helpful person!”.
  3. Kill the Temple Guardian (level 30) in the dungeon. It is immune to magic, so use ranged or melee.
  4. Return to King Roald. Dialogue: “About that job I'm doing”.
  5. Return to the temple (bring a bucket and 50 rune or pure essence).
  6. Kill a Monk of Zamorak (level 30) for a golden key. You can safespot using the pews.
  7. Talk to Drezel on the top floor of the temple (he is locked in a cell). Dialogue: “So, what now?” → “Yes, of course”.
  8. Go down to the underground of the temple through the trapdoor.
  9. Go to the central room and study the monuments to find which has a key on it. Use the golden key on it to get an iron key.
  10. Use the bucket on the well in the central room.
  11. Go back up to the top floor of the temple (with the water).
  12. Open Drezel's cell door with the iron key.
  13. Talk to Drezel to bless the water.
  14. Use the blessed water on the coffin in the same room and leave when Drezel appears.
  15. Talk to Drezel again.
  16. Go down to the underground and talk to Drezel in the east of the underground temple area.
  17. Bring Drezel 50 unnoted rune or pure essence: hand it over in batches that fit your inventory; he accepts it as it comes. Essence pouches help here.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Speak to King Roald in Varrock Castle. Dialogue: “I'm looking for a quest!” → “Yes”.
  - Go to the temple
      1. Go to the temple east of Varrock by the river and click on the large door. Dialogue: “I'll get going” → “Roald sent me to check on Drezel” → “Sure. I'm a helpful person!”.
      2. Kill the Temple Guardian (level 30) in the dungeon. It is immune to magic, so use ranged or melee.
      3. Return to King Roald. Dialogue: “About that job I'm doing”.
  - Return to the temple
      1. Return to the temple (bring a bucket and 50 rune or pure essence).
      2. Kill a Monk of Zamorak (level 30) for a golden key. You can safespot using the pews.
      3. Talk to Drezel on the top floor of the temple (he is locked in a cell). Dialogue: “So, what now?” → “Yes, of course”.
  - Free Drezel
      1. Go down to the underground of the temple through the trapdoor.
      2. Go to the central room and study the monuments to find which has a key on it. Use the golden key on it to get an iron key.
      3. Use the bucket on the well in the central room.
      4. Go back up to the top floor of the temple (with the water).
      5. Open Drezel's cell door with the iron key.
      6. Talk to Drezel to bless the water.
      7. Use the blessed water on the coffin in the same room and leave when Drezel appears.
      8. Talk to Drezel again.
  - Holy Salve
      1. Go down to the underground and talk to Drezel in the east of the underground temple area.
      2. Bring Drezel 50 unnoted rune or pure essence: hand it over in batches that fit your inventory; he accepts it as it comes. Essence pouches help here.

### S8-02 - Nature Spirit

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type quest; members only; 2 quest points
- Comes after: S8-01
- Requirements:
  - Quest: Priest in Peril
  - Quest: The Restless Ghost
- Map: Drezel under the Paterdomus temple (3440, 9894, plane 0)
- NPC: Drezel - The basement of the Paterdomus temple at the north end of the River Salve, Ground floor
- Requirements: Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost.
- Combat: Three ghasts (Ghast, level 30) in the Mort Myre swamp: you can see them only after the blessing. Ghasts turn the food in your inventory into rot: bring 15 lobsters to spare.
- Reward: 2 QP, 3,000 Crafting XP, 2,000 Defence XP, 2,000 Hitpoints XP, access to the Mort Myre swamp.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Fights in melee: Ghast
- Items required:
  - Silver sickle x1 - Buy on the Grand Exchange (about 150-250 gp) or cast from a silver bar in the Sickle mould (Crafting 18). [obtained during the step; item id 2961]
  - Ghostspeak amulet x1 - From The Restless Ghost. [item id 552]
- Items recommended:
  - Lobster x15 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp (20 of them about 2,100). Or catch them yourself from Fishing 40 with a Lobster pot (20 gp in the shop Gerrant's Fishy Business in Port Sarim) at the Musa Point dock on Karamja (boat from Port Sarim, 30 gp) and cook them from Cooking 40 on the range in Al Kharid. [heals 12; item id 379]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Drezel, Filliman Tarlock, Ghast
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Silver sickle, Ghostspeak amulet, Mort myre fungus, Druid pouch
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Well, what is it, I may be able to help?” → “Yes.” → “I'd better get going.” → “How long have you been a ghost?” → “How can I help?” → “I think I've solved the puzzle!”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Nature Spirit
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Drezel under the Paterdomus Temple (Priest in Peril must be complete). Dialogue: “Well, what is it, I may be able to help?” → “Yes”.
  2. Enter Mort Myre from the north gate.
  3. Attempt to enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre; you are not let in. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
  4. Talk to Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
  5. Pick up the washing bowl next to the Grotto.
  6. Pick up the mirror next to the Grotto.
  7. Use the mirror on Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
  8. Right-click search the Grotto tree for the journal.
  9. Use the journal on Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How can I help?”.
  10. Return to Drezel under the Paterdomus Temple to get blessed.
  11. Cast the druidic spell next to a rotten log in Mort Myre to grow a mushroom, and pick it. If you already have, open the quest journal to re-sync your state. Dialogue: “Could I have another bloom scroll please?”.
  12. Use the mushroom on the brown stone outside the Grotto. If you already have, search it instead.
  13. Use the used spell on the gray stone outside the Grotto. If you already have, search it instead.
  14. Stand on the orange stone outside the Grotto.
  15. Tell Filliman Tarlock you're ready. Dialogue: “I think I've solved the puzzle!”.
  16. Enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
  17. Search the altar inside the Grotto.
  18. Talk to Filliman in the Grotto to bless your silver sickle.
  19. Talk to the Nature Spirit in the Grotto to bless your sickle.
  20. Right-click 'Bloom' the blessed sickle next to rotten logs in Mort Myre for Mort Myre fungi. Use these to fill the druid pouch.
  21. Use the filled druid pouch on a ghast to make it attackable and kill it. You'll need to kill 3.
  22. Enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
  23. Talk to the Nature Spirit in the Grotto to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Drezel under the Paterdomus Temple (Priest in Peril must be complete). Dialogue: “Well, what is it, I may be able to help?” → “Yes”.
      2. Enter Mort Myre from the north gate.
      3. Attempt to enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre; you are not let in. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
      4. Talk to Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
      5. Pick up the washing bowl next to the Grotto.
      6. Pick up the mirror next to the Grotto.
      7. Use the mirror on Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How long have you been a ghost?”.
      8. Right-click search the Grotto tree for the journal.
      9. Use the journal on Filliman Tarlock. Dialogue: “How can I help?”.
  - Help Filliman
      1. Return to Drezel under the Paterdomus Temple to get blessed.
      2. Cast the druidic spell next to a rotten log in Mort Myre to grow a mushroom, and pick it. If you already have, open the quest journal to re-sync your state. Dialogue: “Could I have another bloom scroll please?”.
      3. Use the mushroom on the brown stone outside the Grotto. If you already have, search it instead.
      4. Use the used spell on the gray stone outside the Grotto. If you already have, search it instead.
      5. Stand on the orange stone outside the Grotto.
      6. Tell Filliman Tarlock you're ready. Dialogue: “I think I've solved the puzzle!”.
      7. Enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
      8. Search the altar inside the Grotto.
      9. Talk to Filliman in the Grotto to bless your silver sickle.
      10. Talk to the Nature Spirit in the Grotto to bless your sickle.
  - Kill the ghasts
      1. Right-click 'Bloom' the blessed sickle next to rotten logs in Mort Myre for Mort Myre fungi. Use these to fill the druid pouch.
      2. Use the filled druid pouch on a ghast to make it attackable and kill it. You'll need to kill 3.
      3. Enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
      4. Talk to the Nature Spirit in the Grotto to finish the quest.

  - Value 0 and above:
      Go to: Drezel — under the Paterdomus Temple (3439, 9896, plane 0), NPC Drezel

### S8-03 - Agility to 40 (the Graceful outfit is optional)

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type skill; members only; optional
- Map: Al Kharid Rooftop Course (Agility 20-30) (3273, 3195, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Al Kharid Rooftop Course (Agility 20-30) (3273, 3195, plane 0)
  - Varrock Rooftop Course (Agility 30-40) (3220, 3414, plane 0)
- Optional: Nothing later in the route needs Agility above 25 (The Grand Tree, S7-04). Agility 40 and the Graceful outfit only make running cheaper: do it when you want it, not before the quests that follow.
- Courses: 1-20: Gnome Stronghold Agility Course or Draynor Village Rooftop Course (both from level 1); 20-30: Al Kharid Rooftop Course; 30-40: Varrock Rooftop Course; from 40: Canifis Rooftop Course (needs Priest in Peril). The whole plan to 99 is on the Agility skill page.
- Graceful: The set is bought from Grace in the Rogues' Den under the pub in Burthorpe for Marks of Grace: the full set is 260 marks. It weighs almost nothing, and the full set speeds up run energy recovery by 30%. Most marks are on the Canifis course.
- Done when: Agility 40. Graceful pieces and Marks of Grace are a bonus, not a condition.
- Items recommended:
  - Mark of Grace xAs many as you find - Pick them up on the roofs of the courses. [item id 11849]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Grace
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: agility 40

### S8-04 - Ranged to 30, Slayer to 18, Crafting to 31, Woodcutting to 36 and Cooking to 10

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type skill; members only
- Where: Five places in turn: cows in the Lumbridge pasture for Ranged, the Varrock Museum and Draynor Village for the quick Slayer XP, any furnace (Edgeville, Al Kharid or Falador) for Crafting, the Draynor Village willows for Woodcutting and any range for Cooking.
- Map: Varrock Museum — Orlando Smith's quiz in the basement (Slayer) (3255, 3449, plane 0)
- Other places on the map:
  - Varrock Museum — Orlando Smith's quiz in the basement (Slayer) (3255, 3449, plane 0)
  - Draynor Village — the Porcine of Interest notice board (Slayer), willows (Woodcutting) (3103, 3265, plane 0)
  - The cow field east of Lumbridge (Ranged) (3257, 3272, plane 0)
- Why: Animal Magnetism (S8-05) needs Ranged 30, Slayer 18, Crafting 19 and Woodcutting 35; Lost City (S9-01) needs Crafting 31 and Woodcutting 36; Recipe for Disaster (S9-05) needs Cooking 10. The route up to here leaves Woodcutting at 15 and Cooking at 4 (stage 6 is only the optional Bond branch), so this step trains all five. It unlocks Animal Magnetism (Ava's device), Lost City (Zanaris and the Dramen staff) and the Culinaromancer's Chest.
- Ranged 30: Shoot cows in the Lumbridge pasture from a distance with a bow and arrows set to the Accurate style. Use the best bow your level allows: Oak shortbow (level 5), Willow shortbow (level 20), Maple shortbow (level 30), with Iron or Steel arrows first, Mithril arrows from 20. Bring 200-300 arrows and the bow you will use, and pick the arrows up as you go. Stop at 30 for Animal Magnetism; train to 50 only if you want Ava's accumulator (the S8-05 tip).
- Slayer 18: Take the cheap XP first: the Varrock Museum quiz (talk to Orlando Smith, basement) gives 1,000 XP and the short quest A Porcine of Interest (the notice board in Draynor Village) another 1,000, which is level 13. For the rest do one or two tasks from the master Mazchna in Canifis (after Priest in Peril); Turael in Burthorpe gives short tasks with little XP. Stop at 18.
- Crafting 31: Nature Spirit (S8-02) has already given 3,000 XP. From there make jewellery at a furnace with the mould in the bag: gold amulets from Crafting 8 (30 XP each), sapphire rings from 20 (40 XP each), emerald rings from 27 (55 XP each). Buy gold bars and cut gems on the Grand Exchange rather than mining them. Stop at 31.
- Woodcutting 36: Cut oaks (level 15) and then willows (level 30) beside the Draynor Village bank: about 38 XP per oak log and 68 per willow log, and the logs can be sold or burned for Firemaking. 36 needs about 22,000 XP above level 15, roughly 330 willow logs. Use the best axe your level allows.
- Cooking 10: Cook what you already hold on a range or a fire: level 10 is 1,154 XP and Cook's Assistant already gave 300, so 854 XP is left. Cook raw shrimps (30 XP each, about 29 successful cooks) or raw sardines (40 XP each, about 22; both from level 1), or herring from level 5 (50 XP). Trout needs Cooking 15 and cannot be cooked yet. Bring 10-15 more than that: some burn.
- Order: Do Slayer first (it needs no money), then Woodcutting and Cooking (they pay for themselves), then Crafting while you wait for arrows to restock, then Ranged. Check the levels with the Skills page: the goals above are the only targets of this step.
- Done when: Ranged 30, Slayer 18, Crafting 31, Woodcutting 36, Cooking 10.
- Shows the ranged gear advice for your levels and coins
- Items recommended:
  - Oak shortbow x1 - Buy on the Grand Exchange. Ranged 5. Replace it with a Willow shortbow at 20 and a Maple shortbow at 30. [item id 843]
  - Iron arrow x300 - Buy on the Grand Exchange; pick them up again as you shoot. Mithril arrows from Ranged 20. [item id 884]
  - Gold bar x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange: for the gold amulets and rings of the Crafting part. [item id 2357]
  - Ring mould x1 - General Store or the Grand Exchange. [item id 1592]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Varrock Museum quiz: talk to Orlando Smith in the basement (1,000 Slayer XP). [you tick it]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): A Porcine of Interest (optional quest): read the notice board in Draynor Village, talk to Sarah at the South Falador Farm, use a rope on the Strange Hole east of Draynor Manor, then kill Sourhog (level 37) and cut off its foot. Bring a rope, a knife or slash weapon, combat gear and food. +1 QP. [checked from the quest list: A Porcine of Interest]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Orlando Smith, Mazchna, Turael
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: ranged 30, slayer 18, crafting 31, woodcutting 36, cooking 10

### S8-05 - Animal Magnetism

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type quest; members only; 1 quest point
- Comes after: S8-01, S8-04
- Requirements:
  - Quest: The Restless Ghost
  - Quest: Ernest the Chicken
  - Quest: Priest in Peril
  - slayer 18
  - crafting 19
  - ranged 30
  - woodcutting 35
- Map: Ava in Draynor Manor (3094, 3357, plane 0)
- NPC: Ava - Draynor Manor, Ground floor
- Pro tip: With 50 Ranged the reward is Ava's accumulator: it returns 72% of fired arrows straight to the quiver. Below 50 you get Ava's attractor, which is worse. If your Ranged is almost 50, train it before handing in the quest.
- Requirements: The Restless Ghost, Ernest the Chicken, Priest in Peril; Slayer 18, Crafting 19, Ranged 30, Woodcutting 35.
- Reward: 1 QP, 1,000 Crafting XP, 1,000 Fletching XP, 1,000 Slayer XP, 2,500 Woodcutting XP, Ava's accumulator (with 50 Ranged) or Ava's attractor.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Mithril axe x1 - Only a mithril one: no other axe will do. About 150 gp. [item id 1355]
  - Iron bar x5 - Not as banknotes. [item id 2351]
  - Hammer x1 - Buy at any General Store for 1 gp. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 2347]
  - Ghostspeak amulet x1 - From The Restless Ghost. [item id 552]
  - Holy symbol x1 - About 170 gp on the Grand Exchange. [item id 1718]
  - Polished buttons x1 - They drop in the H.A.M. sewers or buy on the Grand Exchange. [obtained during the step; item id 10496]
  - Hard leather x1 - Have a cowhide tanned by the tanner (3 gp) or buy one (about 180 gp). [item id 1743]
  - Ecto-token x20 - Get them at the Ectofuntus in Port Phasmatys (4 bones + 4 buckets + 4 pots): Prayer levels along the way. [item id 4278]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Wear Ava's accumulator (needs 50 Ranged) or Ava's attractor, whichever the quest gave you. [checked from the worn items: Ava's accumulator, Ava's attractor]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Ava, Alice, Old crone, Malcolm, Turael
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Mithril axe, Iron bar, Hammer, Ghostspeak amulet, Holy symbol, Polished buttons, Hard leather, Ecto-token
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “I would be happy to make your home into a better place.” → “I'm here about a quest.” → “I'm here about the farmers east of here.” → “Okay, you need it more than I do, I suppose.” → “Could I buy those chickens now?” → “Could I buy two chickens?” → “Hello, I'm here about those trees again.” → “I'd love one, thanks.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Animal Magnetism
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Ava to begin the quest. Dialogue: “I would be happy to make your home a better place”.
  2. Talk to Malcolm at the farm west of the Ectofuntus.
  3. Talk to Alice. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest”.
  4. Talk to Malcolm again.
  5. Talk to Alice again. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest”.
  6. Talk to the Old crone just east of the Slayer Tower twice. Dialogue: “I'm here about the farmers east of here”.
  7. Give the Crone-made amulet to Malcolm. Dialogue: “Okay, you need it more than I do, I suppose”.
  8. Talk to Malcolm again to watch a cutscene.
  9. Buy two undead chickens from Malcolm. You can acquire ecto-tokens using the Ectofuntus to the east. Dialogue: “Could I buy those chickens now, then?” → “Could I buy 2 chickens?”.
  10. Give the two undead chickens to Ava.
  11. Talk to the witch in the manor twice.
  12. Go to the iron mine in Rimmington north-east of the house portal.
  13. While looking north, use the hammer on the selected iron ore.
  14. Give the bar magnet to Ava.
  15. Try to chop an undead tree outside Draynor Manor.
  16. Talk to Turael in Burthorpe twice, giving him the mithril axe and holy symbol. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest” → “Hello, I'm here about those trees again” → “I'd love one, thanks”.
  17. Chop an undead tree outside Draynor Manor with the blessed axe until you receive undead twigs.
  18. Give the undead twigs to Ava.
  19. Talk to Ava to receive the research notes.
  20. Translate the research notes: click all the highlighted switches.
  21. Give Ava the translated research notes.
  22. Combine hard leather and polished buttons with the pattern.
  23. Give Ava the container.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to Ava to begin the quest. Dialogue: “I would be happy to make your home a better place”.
  - Undead chickens
      1. Talk to Malcolm at the farm west of the Ectofuntus.
      2. Talk to Alice. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest”.
      3. Talk to Malcolm again.
      4. Talk to Alice again. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest”.
      5. Talk to the Old crone just east of the Slayer Tower twice. Dialogue: “I'm here about the farmers east of here”.
      6. Give the Crone-made amulet to Malcolm. Dialogue: “Okay, you need it more than I do, I suppose”.
      7. Talk to Malcolm again to watch a cutscene.
      8. Buy two undead chickens from Malcolm. You can acquire ecto-tokens using the Ectofuntus to the east. Dialogue: “Could I buy those chickens now, then?” → “Could I buy 2 chickens?”.
      9. Give the two undead chickens to Ava.
  - The magnet
      1. Talk to the witch in the manor twice.
      2. Go to the iron mine in Rimmington north-east of the house portal.
      3. While looking north, use the hammer on the selected iron ore.
      4. Give the bar magnet to Ava.
  - Undead twigs
      1. Try to chop an undead tree outside Draynor Manor.
      2. Talk to Turael in Burthorpe twice, giving him the mithril axe and holy symbol. Dialogue: “I'm here about a quest” → “Hello, I'm here about those trees again” → “I'd love one, thanks”.
      3. Chop an undead tree outside Draynor Manor with the blessed axe until you receive undead twigs.
      4. Give the undead twigs to Ava.
      5. Talk to Ava to receive the research notes.
      6. Translate the research notes: click all the highlighted switches.
      7. Give Ava the translated research notes.
      8. Combine hard leather and polished buttons with the pattern.
      9. Give Ava the container.

### S8-06 - Prayer to 43 (Protect from Melee)

- stage 8 (Global transport and stamina); type skill; members only
- Where: The Ectofuntus in Port Phasmatys (Morytania). The bank is in Port Phasmatys, a few steps from the temple.
- Map: The Ectofuntus, Port Phasmatys (3660, 3520, plane 0)
- How: Buy the bones, grind them, fill buckets with slime, and worship the Ectofuntus. Each offering gives four times the Prayer XP of burying the same bone.
- Why: Monkey Madness I (S9-04) asks for Protect from Melee all through the Ape Atoll dungeon and Protect from Magic against the Jungle Demon, which hits up to 32 with both magic and melee. Protect from Melee needs Prayer 43, so this level is a requirement of that quest, not a bonus.
- Quest XP first: The Restless Ghost, Priest in Peril, Recruitment Drive and Holy Grail give 14,531 Prayer XP together, which is level 30. Two of them are already on your route. Holy Grail alone gives 11,000 Prayer XP and 15,300 Defence XP (after Merlin's Crystal, with Attack 20 for Excalibur, and a level 120 Black Knight Titan to beat), so do it first if you want to save the bones. The two lamps (X Marks the Spot, The Ides of Milk) are already counted.
- Default method: Offer bones at the Ectofuntus: safe, no skill requirement and four times the XP of burying. A bone gives 18 XP, a Big bone 60 and a Dragon bone 288. About 180 offerings an hour: Big bones are about 10,800 XP an hour, Dragon bones about 52,000. Dragon bones are the fast way and cost more, Big bones the cheap and slow one.
- How an offering goes: Use a bucket on the pool of slime (it fills every bucket in the bag). Go upstairs and use the bones on the bone grinder loader, not bury them: it makes bonemeal. Click the Ectofuntus with bonemeal and a bucket of slime to worship. A ghost disciple pays 5 ecto-tokens for each offering while you wear the Ghostspeak amulet.
- Alternative: Offering plain bones at the Chaos altar in the Wilderness is cheaper per XP, but other players can attack you there: it is not part of this route.
- Done when: Prayer 43.
- Items required:
  - Ghostspeak amulet x1 - From The Restless Ghost (S1-06). Worn, it lets you talk to the ghost disciple who pays the ecto-tokens. [item id 552]
  - Bones x9 - Per trip. Use Big bones (60 XP each) or Dragon bones (288 XP each) instead: buy them on the Grand Exchange, the live price is in the app. Keep the Big bones from S4-03 if you still have them. [item id 526]
  - Pot x9 - Per trip: they hold the bonemeal. General Store, 1 gp. [item id 1931]
  - Bucket x9 - Per trip: they hold the slime. General Store, 2 gp. [item id 1925]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Ghost disciple
  - Objects highlighted: Ectofuntus, Pool of Slime, Loader
  - Marked done automatically when: levels reached: prayer 43

## Stage 9 - Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves

### S9-01 - Lost City

- stage 9 (Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves); type quest; members only; 3 quest points
- Comes after: S8-04
- Requirements:
  - crafting 31 (can be boosted)
  - woodcutting 36 (can be boosted)
- Map: The Lost City adventurers' camp (3150, 3205, plane 0)
- NPC: Warrior - The adventurers' camp in the north-west corner of Lumbridge Swamp, Ground floor
- Requirements: Crafting 31, Woodcutting 36.
- Entrana: You cannot take weapons or armour to the island of Entrana: only food and runes for magic. Leave the axe in the bank: you will find a knife and an axe along the way.
- Safespot: The Tree spirit (level 101) can be killed by hiding behind a mushroom and using a bow or magic.
- Reward: 3 QP, access to the hidden city of Zanaris (Dramen staff), the right to wear the Dragon dagger and Dragon longsword (with 60 Attack), Cosmic rune crafting.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Knife x1 - To carve the staff from a Dramen branch. [item id 946]
  - Bronze axe x1 - Drops from the zombies in the Entrana dungeon: do not take an axe with you. [item id 1351]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Warrior, Shamus, Tree spirit
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Knife, Bronze axe, Dramen branch, Dramen staff
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “What are you camped here for?” → “Who's Zanaris?” → “If it's hidden how are you planning to find it?” → “Looks like you don't know either.” → “How does it fit in a shed then?”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Lost City
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to the Warrior south-east of Draynor Village. Dialogue: “What are you camped out here for?” → “What makes you think it's out here?” → “If it's hidden how are you planning to find it?” → “Looks like you don't know either” → “Yes”.
  2. Try cutting the tree just to the west; a leprechaun appears. Dialogue: “I've been in that shed, I didn't see a city”.
  3. Talk to Shamus. Dialogue: “I've been in that shed, I didn't see a city”.
  4. Bank all weapons and armour you have (including the axe), go to Port Sarim and take the boat to Entrana.
  5. Climb down the ladder on the north side of the island. Once you go down, you can only escape via teleport. Dialogue: “Well that is a risk I will have to take”.
  6. Kill zombies until one drops an axe.
  7. Attempt to cut a branch from the Dramen tree. Be prepared for a Tree Spirit (level 101) to appear, which you can safespot behind nearby fungus.
  8. Kill the Tree Spirit. It can be safespotted behind the fungi to the east.
  9. Cut at least one branch from the Dramen tree. It's recommended you cut at least 4 branches so you don't have to return in future quests.
  10. Teleport away with the branches, preferably to Lumbridge.
  11. Use a knife on the Dramen branch to craft a Dramen staff.
  12. Enter the shed south of Lumbridge with your Dramen staff equipped; this is the entrance to Zanaris.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to the Warrior south-east of Draynor Village. Dialogue: “What are you camped out here for?” → “What makes you think it's out here?” → “If it's hidden how are you planning to find it?” → “Looks like you don't know either” → “Yes”.
  - Find Shamus
      1. Try cutting the tree just to the west; a leprechaun appears. Dialogue: “I've been in that shed, I didn't see a city”.
      2. Talk to Shamus. Dialogue: “I've been in that shed, I didn't see a city”.
  - Dramen branch
      1. Bank all weapons and armour you have (including the axe), go to Port Sarim and take the boat to Entrana.
      2. Climb down the ladder on the north side of the island. Once you go down, you can only escape via teleport. Dialogue: “Well that is a risk I will have to take”.
      3. Kill zombies until one drops an axe.
      4. Attempt to cut a branch from the Dramen tree. Be prepared for a Tree Spirit (level 101) to appear, which you can safespot behind nearby fungus.
      5. Kill the Tree Spirit. It can be safespotted behind the fungi to the east.
      6. Cut at least one branch from the Dramen tree. It's recommended you cut at least 4 branches so you don't have to return in future quests.
      7. Teleport away with the branches, preferably to Lumbridge.
  - Enter Zanaris
      1. Use a knife on the Dramen branch to craft a Dramen staff.
      2. Enter the shed south of Lumbridge with your Dramen staff equipped; this is the entrance to Zanaris.

### S9-02 - Fairytale I - Growing Pains

- stage 9 (Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves); type quest; members only; 2 quest points
- Comes after: S9-01, S8-02
- Requirements:
  - Quest: Lost City
  - Quest: Nature Spirit
- Map: Martin the Master Gardener, the Draynor Village market (3078, 3258, plane 0)
- NPC: Martin the Master Gardener - The Draynor Village market, Ground floor
- Requirements: Lost City, Nature Spirit (and so Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost).
- Combat: Tanglefoot (level 111): he can be damaged only with the Magic secateurs, which are given during the quest. Bring 10 lobsters.
- Reward: 2 QP, the Magic secateurs, 3,500 Farming XP, 2,000 Attack XP, 1,000 Magic XP.
- Done when: The quest is green.
- Items required:
  - Secateurs x1 - From Sarah in the farmer's shop north-west of the quest start for 5 gp. [from: Sarah; item id 5329]
  - Spade x1 - Buy in the Lumbridge General Store for 3 gp. [from: Lumbridge General Store; item id 952]
  - Ghostspeak amulet x1 - From The Restless Ghost. [item id 552]
  - Dramen staff x1 - From Lost City. [item id 772]
- Items recommended:
  - Lobster x10 - Buy on the Grand Exchange for about 105 gp (20 of them about 2,100). Or catch them yourself from Fishing 40 with a Lobster pot (20 gp in the shop Gerrant's Fishy Business in Port Sarim) at the Musa Point dock on Karamja (boat from Port Sarim, 30 gp) and cook them from Cooking 40 on the range in Al Kharid. [heals 12; item id 379]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Martin the Master Gardener, Fairy Godfather, Malignius Mortifer, Elstan, Frizzy Skernip, Bolongo, Prissy Scilla, Heskel
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Secateurs, Spade, Ghostspeak amulet, Dramen staff, Magic secateurs
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Quest.” → “Anything I can help with?” → “Now that I think about it, you're right!” → “Are you a member of the Group of Advanced Gardeners?” → “Where's the Fairy Queen?” → “I need help with fighting a Tanglefoot.”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Fairytale I - Growing Pains
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest” → “Anything I can help with?” → “Now that I think about it, ” → “you're right!”.
  2. Talk to 5 farmers: Frizzy Yimshaw in Port Sarim, Elstan north-west of Draynor, Heskel in Falador Park, Treznor south of Varrock Castle and Dreven south of Varrock by the Champions' Guild. You can buy secateurs from Sarah at the farm south of Falador. Dialogue: “Are you a member of the Group of Advanced Gardeners?”.
  3. Return to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest”.
  4. Travel to Zanaris (Dramen staff in hand; the entrance is the shed south of Lumbridge).
  5. Talk to the Fairy Godfather in the Throne Room. Dialogue: “Where's the Fairy Queen?”.
  6. Talk to Fairy Nuff in north-west Zanaris.
  7. Talk to Zandar in the Dark Wizards' Tower west of Falador; climb to the top floor.
  8. Dig for a skull in the north of Draynor Manor (you need a spade).
  9. Talk to Malignius Mortifer west of Port Sarim. Dialogue: “I need help with fighting a Tanglefoot” → “I was asking you about fighting a Tanglefoot”.
  10. Get the items Mortifer tells you to get, and enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
  11. Talk to the Nature Spirit; he enchants the secateurs.
  12. Travel to Zanaris, ready to fight the Tanglefoot.
  13. Enter the Tanglefoot lair in the south of Zanaris, near the cosmic altar.
  14. Kill the large Tanglefoot with the magic secateurs; only they damage it. You can flinch it on a corner.
  15. Talk to the Fairy Godfather to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Investigation
      1. Talk to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest” → “Anything I can help with?” → “Now that I think about it, ” → “you're right!”.
      2. Talk to 5 farmers: Frizzy Yimshaw in Port Sarim, Elstan north-west of Draynor, Heskel in Falador Park, Treznor south of Varrock Castle and Dreven south of Varrock by the Champions' Guild. You can buy secateurs from Sarah at the farm south of Falador. Dialogue: “Are you a member of the Group of Advanced Gardeners?”.
      3. Return to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest”.
  - Strange events
      1. Travel to Zanaris (Dramen staff in hand; the entrance is the shed south of Lumbridge).
      2. Talk to the Fairy Godfather in the Throne Room. Dialogue: “Where's the Fairy Queen?”.
      3. Talk to Fairy Nuff in north-west Zanaris.
      4. Talk to Zandar in the Dark Wizards' Tower west of Falador; climb to the top floor.
  - Find the cure
      1. Dig for a skull in the north of Draynor Manor (you need a spade).
      2. Talk to Malignius Mortifer west of Port Sarim. Dialogue: “I need help with fighting a Tanglefoot” → “I was asking you about fighting a Tanglefoot”.
  - Enchant the secateurs
      1. Get the items Mortifer tells you to get, and enter the Grotto in the south of Mort Myre.
      2. Talk to the Nature Spirit; he enchants the secateurs.
  - Defeat the Tanglefoot
      1. Travel to Zanaris, ready to fight the Tanglefoot.
      2. Enter the Tanglefoot lair in the south of Zanaris, near the cosmic altar.
      3. Kill the large Tanglefoot with the magic secateurs; only they damage it. You can flinch it on a corner.
      4. Talk to the Fairy Godfather to finish the quest.

### S9-03 - Fairytale II - Cure a Queen (the start only)

- stage 9 (Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves); type quest; members only
- Comes after: S9-02
- Map: Martin the Master Gardener, the Draynor Village market (3078, 3258, plane 0)
- NPC: Martin the Master Gardener - The Draynor Village market (after the first part), Ground floor
- Important: You do not need to complete the whole quest. It is enough to start it with Martin and talk to the Fairy Godfather in Zanaris: the step is marked as soon as the game counts his permission (quest stage 40), and the rings work from then on. Thieving 40, Farming 49 and Herblore 57 are needed only to finish the quest, not for the rings.
- Reward: Access to the Fairy rings portal network! With a Dramen staff worn you can teleport all over the mainland of Gielinor in a couple of seconds.
- Done when: The Fairy Godfather has given you permission to use the fairy rings (the game counts it as quest stage 40). The rest of the quest is not part of the route.
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Martin the Master Gardener, Fairy Godfather, Co-ordinator, Fairy Fixit
  - Objects highlighted: Fairy ring
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Quest.” → “I suppose I'd better go see what the problem is then.” → “Where is the Fairy Queen?” → “Where could she have been taken to?” → “Yes, okay.” → “The fairy rings.” → “Why are you carrying that toolbox?”
  - Marked done automatically when: variable 2326 at least 40
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest”.
  2. Wait 5 minutes.
  3. Return to Martin in the Draynor Market. Dialogue: “Ask about the quest” → “I suppose I'd better go and see what the problem is then”.
  4. Travel to Zanaris.
  5. Search the healing certificate in Fairy Nuff's room.
  6. Right-click study the certificate.
  7. Read the sign near the Cosmic Temple in the south of Zanaris.
  8. Talk to the Fairy Godfather in the Throne Room. Dialogue: “Where is the Fairy Queen?” → “Where could she have been taken to?” → “Yes, okay”.
  9. While carrying Nuff's certificate, use the fairy rings to travel to A.I.R., D.L.R., D.J.Q. then A.J.S.
  10. Talk to Fairy Nuff.
  11. Pickpocket the Fairy Godfather in the Zanaris Throne Room.
  12. Give Fairy Nuff the Queen's secateurs.
  13. Return to Fairy Nuff in the hideout.
  14. Travel to C.K.P. with a fairy ring. Even if you've already made a magic essence (unf), you'll need to make another.
  15. Wait for a star flower to appear.
  16. Pick a star flower.
  17. Travel to D.I.R. with a fairy ring, ready to kill a Gorak. Goraks can hit through protection prayers.
  18. Kill Goraks for their claws.
  19. Pick up the Gorak claws.
  20. Add the star flower to a vial of water.
  21. Use a pestle and mortar on the Gorak claws.
  22. Add the Gorak claw powder to the unfinished potion.
  23. While carrying Nuff's certificate, use the fairy rings to travel to A.I.R., D.L.R., D.J.Q. then A.J.S.
  24. Use the magic essence on the Fairy Queen.
  25. Talk to the Fairy Queen to finish the quest.

### S9-04 - Monkey Madness I

- stage 9 (Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves); type quest; members only; 3 quest points
- Comes after: S7-03, S7-05, S8-06
- Requirements:
  - Quest: The Grand Tree
  - Quest: Tree Gnome Village
  - prayer 43
- Map: King Narnode Shareen, Grand Tree (2466, 3496, plane 0)
- NPC: King Narnode Shareen - Tree Gnome Stronghold, Ground floor
- Requirements: Tree Gnome Village and The Grand Tree (S7-03, S7-05), Prayer 43 for Protect from Melee (S8-06) and the ability to beat a level 195 Jungle Demon: it hits up to 32 with magic and melee, and only a protection prayer stops it.
- Before you leave the bank: Gold bar, Ball of wool and a Lockpick are used at Zooknock and on the island, where there is no bank: take them now. 5 free slots, 4 Prayer potions, an Antipoison and 15 Lobsters. A Knife is worth taking: pineapples heal for free. During the quest you pick up monkey bones on Karamja and 5 bananas on Ape Atoll yourself.
- Dangers: Monkey Zombies hit up to 7, Monkey Skeletons up to 12, and the spikes, rock traps and falling rocks of the dungeon take a share of your current health: move through the dungeon with Protect from Melee on and eat before each room.
- Safespot: The Jungle demon (level 195) hits in melee and with magic: turn on Protect from Magic, stay three or more squares away so its melee cannot reach you, and hit it with Ranged (it is weakest to Ranged) from the far side.
- Reward: 3 QP, 10,000 Coins, 3 diamonds, Daero's training (choose: 35,000 XP in Attack and Defence plus 20,000 in Strength and Hitpoints, or the other way round) and the right to wear the Dragon scimitar with 60 Attack.
- The reward is a choice: the program shows the option that fits your levels (daero).
- Done when: The quest is green and a Dragon scimitar is bought.
- Items required:
  - Gold bar x1 - Waterfall Quest (S7-01) gives 2 gold bars. Otherwise buy one on the Grand Exchange or smelt it (Smithing 40). Zooknock needs it for the M'speak amulet. [item id 2357]
  - Ball of wool x1 - Spin wool at a spinning wheel or buy one on the Grand Exchange. It strings the M'speak amulet. [item id 1759]
  - Lockpick x1 - Thieving shop or the Grand Exchange. It opens your jail cell on the island. [item id 1523]
- Items recommended:
  - Prayer potion(4) x4 - On the Grand Exchange. Keeps Protect from Melee up against the monkeys and the demon. [item id 2434]
  - Antipoison(4) x1 - On the Grand Exchange. There are poisonous snakes and spiders on the island. [item id 2446]
  - Lobster x15 - Buy on the Grand Exchange, or any better food you already have. A multi-combat area of strong monkeys: eat before each room. [any of: Trout, Salmon, Tuna, Swordfish, Monkfish, Shark, Cooked meat, Cake, Pie; heals 12; item id 379]
  - Knife x1 - Cut pineapples on Ape Atoll for free healing. [item id 946]
  - Energy potion(4) x2 - The route is long on foot: you may not teleport during the audience with Awowogei. [item id 3008]
- Inventories by phase:
  - The Grand Tree and Karamja, keep 2 slots free - Leave room for the gnome royal seal and the king's orders: without a free slot they drop on the ground. Monkey bones are picked up on Karamja.
      take Lobster x15 - or any food
      take Antipoison(4) x1
      take Lockpick x1
      take Gold bar x1
      take Ball of wool x1
  - Crash Island and Ape Atoll - Keep Protect from Melee on. Heal before the crate fall in the south-east: it hurts. At Zooknock you must hold the monkey dentures, the M'amulet mould, the Ball of wool and the Gold bar.
      take Prayer potion(4) x4
      take Lobster x15
      take Antipoison(4) x1
      take Lockpick x1
  - Temple of Marimbo and the talismans - String the amulet with the wool, then wear it to talk to the monkey child.
      take M'amulet mould x1 - found on the island
      take Enchanted bar x1 - from Zooknock
      take Ball of wool x1
      take Banana x5 - from the garden trees
  - The Jungle Demon - Protect from Magic, and stay three or more squares away: its melee reaches two squares. It hits up to 32 and is weakest to Ranged. Do not use any teleport on the walk back for the audience with Awowogei.
      take 10th squad sigil x1 - equip it only when you are ready
      take Prayer potion(4) x4
      take Lobster x10 - or better food
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Reach Attack 60: the Dragon scimitar cannot be wielded below it. Daero's training takes you to about Attack 52 at most; the rest is yours to train and is not a step of the route. [checked from the level: attack 60]
- Also tracked (does not block the step): Wield the Dragon scimitar. [checked from the worn items: Dragon scimitar]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: King Narnode Shareen, Daero, Waydar
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Yes” → “Talk about the journey...” → “Return to previous menu” → “Talk about the 1-th squad...” → “Talk about Caranock...” → “Leave...” → “Who is it?” → “I cannot convince Lumdo to take us to the island.” → “Yes, I'm sure.” → “Well I'll be a monkey's uncle!” → “How many bananas did Aunty want?” → “I've lost that toy you gave me...” → “Wow - can I borrow it?” → “Ok, I promise!”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Monkey Madness I and holding 1 x Dragon scimitar
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  2. Fly with Captain Errdo to Gandius.
  3. Talk to G.L.O. Caranock in the shipyard.
  4. Return to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold.
  5. Talk to Daero on the 1st floor of the Tree Gnome Stronghold. Make sure to go through all of the chat options until you are given a 'Leave...' option. Dialogue: “Leave” → “Who is it?” → “Yes” → “Talk about the 10th squad”.
  6. Talk to Daero in the hangar.
  7. Operate the reinitialisation panel and solve the puzzle.
  8. Talk to Daero in the hangar again.
  9. Talk to Waydar. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  10. Talk to Lumdo.
  11. Talk to Waydar on Crash Island. Dialogue: “I cannot convince Lumdo to take us to the island”.
  12. Head west and enter the valley going north WITH PROTECT FROM RANGED ON. Be wary of poison and taking damage.
  13. Wait for the gorilla guard to start going away from the prison cell, then sneak out and go to the north side of the prison.
  14. Stick to the east edge of the town, and make your way to Garkor to the south-east.
  15. Head west and enter the large open building via the south door. DO NOT STAND ON THE LIGHT FLOOR IN THE BUILDING.
  16. DO NOT WALK ON THE LIGHT FLOOR. Search the stacked crates for monkey dentures. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  17. Search the crate in the south-east corner and go down into the cavern. Dialogue: “Yes, I'm sure”.
  18. Search the crate in the north-west of the room for an M'amulet mould. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  19. Teleport out to prepare for a dangerous portion. You'll want energy/stamina potions, food and prayer potions.
  20. Get food, antipoison, energy/stamina/prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
  21. Enter the dungeon in south Ape Atoll.
  22. Talk to Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. Dialogue: “What do we need for the monkey amulet?” → “I'll be back later”.
  23. Use the monkey dentures on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  24. Use the M'amulet mould on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  25. Use the gold bar on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  26. Teleport out to prepare to make the amulet. You'll want some food, prayer potions, antipoisons, the mould and the enchanted bar.
  27. Get food, antipoison, prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
  28. Head west and enter the valley going north WITH PROTECT FROM RANGED ON. Be wary of poison and taking damage.
  29. Wait for the gorilla guard to start going away from the prison cell, then sneak out and go to the north side of the prison. Afterwards, head into the temple's basement.
  30. Use the enchanted bar on the wall of flame.
  31. Climb the rope out. If you plan on doing Recipe for Disaster, also kill a zombie monkey for its bones whilst here, and a gorilla upstairs.
  32. Go to the north-west of the island and talk to the monkey child there a few times. Eventually you'll give him 5 bananas, and he'll give you a monkey talisman. Dialogue: “Well I'll be a monkey's uncle!” → “How many bananas did Aunty want?” → “Ok, I promise!” → “I've lost that toy you gave me” → “Wow - can I borrow it?”.
  33. Talk to the monkey child again.
  34. Talk to the monkey child again.
  35. Give the monkey child 5 bananas.
  36. Wait for the aunt to make another round, then talk to the monkey child for the talisman.
  37. This section will help you get ready for Recipe for Disaster. If you aren't interested in this, tick the box for this section in the sidebar to skip it. Dialogue: “I've lost that toy you gave me” → “Wow - can I borrow it?” → “Ok, I promise!”.
  38. Kill a monkey archer for its bones.
  39. Kill a gorilla in the temple for its bones. If it can attack you it heals on hit, so it's recommended to safespot or flinch one.
  40. Enter the temple's basement to kill a zombie monkey for its bones.
  41. Kill a zombie monkey for its bones.
  42. Teleport out to prepare to make the talisman. You'll want some food, prayer potions and antipoisons.
  43. Get food, antipoison, prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
  44. Enter the dungeon in south Ape Atoll.
  45. Use the monkey talisman on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  46. Use the monkey bones on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  47. If you want to make a zombie, ninja and gorilla greegree for RFD, talk to and give Zooknock the bones/talismans. Once you're done, teleport away for the next step. Dialogue: “Can you make another monkey talisman?” → “Yes”.
  48. Talk to the Monkey Minder in the Ardougne Zoo whilst wielding the Karamjan monkey greegree.
  49. Talk to a monkey in the pen.
  50. UNEQUIP the greegree, then talk to the Monkey Minder again to leave.
  51. WALK/RUN to Daero to return to Ape Atoll. If you teleport, you'll have to start again.
  52. Talk to Garkor.
  53. Talk to a guard outside the building in the south-east of Marim. If you already have, open the quest journal to re-sync.
  54. Talk to Kruk in the north-west of the island.
  55. Talk to Awowogei twice.
  56. Talk to Garkor.
  57. Prepare to fight the Jungle Demon. Once you're ready, equip the 10th squad sigil.
  58. Kill the Jungle Demon. Use Protect from Magic and keep your distance to kill it safely.
  59. Return to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold to finish the quest.
- Quest outline (sections, as in the Quest Helper panel):
  - Getting started
      1. Talk to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold. Dialogue: “Yes”.
  - Inspect the shipyard
      1. Fly with Captain Errdo to Gandius.
      2. Talk to G.L.O. Caranock in the shipyard.
      3. Return to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold.
  - Sail to Ape Atoll
      1. Talk to Daero on the 1st floor of the Tree Gnome Stronghold. Make sure to go through all of the chat options until you are given a 'Leave...' option. Dialogue: “Leave” → “Who is it?” → “Yes” → “Talk about the 10th squad”.
      2. Talk to Daero in the hangar.
      3. Operate the reinitialisation panel and solve the puzzle.
      4. Talk to Daero in the hangar again.
      5. Talk to Waydar. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      6. Talk to Lumdo.
      7. Talk to Waydar on Crash Island. Dialogue: “I cannot convince Lumdo to take us to the island”.
  - Find Garkor
      1. Head west and enter the valley going north WITH PROTECT FROM RANGED ON. Be wary of poison and taking damage.
      2. Wait for the gorilla guard to start going away from the prison cell, then sneak out and go to the north side of the prison.
      3. Stick to the east edge of the town, and make your way to Garkor to the south-east.
  - Amulet parts
      1. Head west and enter the large open building via the south door. DO NOT STAND ON THE LIGHT FLOOR IN THE BUILDING.
      2. DO NOT WALK ON THE LIGHT FLOOR. Search the stacked crates for monkey dentures. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      3. Search the crate in the south-east corner and go down into the cavern. Dialogue: “Yes, I'm sure”.
      4. Search the crate in the north-west of the room for an M'amulet mould. Dialogue: “Yes”.
      5. Teleport out to prepare for a dangerous portion. You'll want energy/stamina potions, food and prayer potions.
  - Enchanted bar
      1. Get food, antipoison, energy/stamina/prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
      2. Enter the dungeon in south Ape Atoll.
      3. Talk to Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. Dialogue: “What do we need for the monkey amulet?” → “I'll be back later”.
      4. Use the monkey dentures on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
      5. Use the M'amulet mould on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
      6. Use the gold bar on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
  - Make the amulet
      1. Teleport out to prepare to make the amulet. You'll want some food, prayer potions, antipoisons, the mould and the enchanted bar.
      2. Get food, antipoison, prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
      3. Head west and enter the valley going north WITH PROTECT FROM RANGED ON. Be wary of poison and taking damage.
      4. Wait for the gorilla guard to start going away from the prison cell, then sneak out and go to the north side of the prison. Afterwards, head into the temple's basement.
      5. Use the enchanted bar on the wall of flame.
  - Talisman
      1. Climb the rope out. If you plan on doing Recipe for Disaster, also kill a zombie monkey for its bones whilst here, and a gorilla upstairs.
      2. Go to the north-west of the island and talk to the monkey child there a few times. Eventually you'll give him 5 bananas, and he'll give you a monkey talisman. Dialogue: “Well I'll be a monkey's uncle!” → “How many bananas did Aunty want?” → “Ok, I promise!” → “I've lost that toy you gave me” → “Wow - can I borrow it?”.
      3. Talk to the monkey child again.
      4. Talk to the monkey child again.
      5. Give the monkey child 5 bananas.
      6. Wait for the aunt to make another round, then talk to the monkey child for the talisman.
  - Bones for RFD (optional)
      1. This section will help you get ready for Recipe for Disaster. If you aren't interested in this, tick the box for this section in the sidebar to skip it. Dialogue: “I've lost that toy you gave me” → “Wow - can I borrow it?” → “Ok, I promise!”.
      2. Kill a monkey archer for its bones.
      3. Kill a gorilla in the temple for its bones. If it can attack you it heals on hit, so it's recommended to safespot or flinch one.
      4. Enter the temple's basement to kill a zombie monkey for its bones.
      5. Kill a zombie monkey for its bones.
  - Make the greegree
      1. Teleport out to prepare to make the talisman. You'll want some food, prayer potions and antipoisons.
      2. Get food, antipoison, prayer potions, and return to Ape Atoll.
      3. Enter the dungeon in south Ape Atoll.
      4. Use the monkey talisman on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
      5. Use the monkey bones on Zooknock in the north-east of the dungeon. If you've already done so, open the quest journal to re-sync your current state.
      6. If you want to make a zombie, ninja and gorilla greegree for RFD, talk to and give Zooknock the bones/talismans. Once you're done, teleport away for the next step. Dialogue: “Can you make another monkey talisman?” → “Yes”.
  - Rescue the monkey
      1. Talk to the Monkey Minder in the Ardougne Zoo whilst wielding the Karamjan monkey greegree.
      2. Talk to a monkey in the pen.
      3. UNEQUIP the greegree, then talk to the Monkey Minder again to leave.
      4. WALK/RUN to Daero to return to Ape Atoll. If you teleport, you'll have to start again.
      5. Talk to Garkor.
      6. Talk to a guard outside the building in the south-east of Marim. If you already have, open the quest journal to re-sync.
      7. Talk to Kruk in the north-west of the island.
      8. Talk to Awowogei twice.
      9. Talk to Garkor.
  - Defeat the demon
      1. Prepare to fight the Jungle Demon. Once you're ready, equip the 10th squad sigil.
      2. Kill the Jungle Demon. Use Protect from Magic and keep your distance to kill it safely.
      3. Return to King Narnode Shareen in the Tree Gnome Stronghold to finish the quest.

### S9-05 - Recipe for Disaster — Another Cook's Quest

- stage 9 (Dragon weapons and the road to Barrows Gloves); type quest; members only; 1 quest point
- Requirements:
  - cooking 10
  - Quest: Cook's Assistant
- Map: The cook's kitchen in Lumbridge Castle (3208, 3214, plane 0)
- NPC: Cook - The Lumbridge Castle kitchen, Ground floor
- How: Complete the first chapter, Another Cook's Quest: bring the cook 4 items, then go into the dining room and watch the cutscene. After it the Culinaromancer's Chest appears in the castle basement.
- Requirements: Cooking 10 and Cook's Assistant.
- Reward: 1 QP, access to the Culinaromancer's Chest: the more chapters of Recipe for Disaster are completed, the better the gloves in the chest, up to the Barrows gloves after all the chapters.
- Done when: The first chapter is closed and the chest in the castle basement is open.
- Items required:
  - Eye of newt x1 - Buy in Betty's shop in Port Sarim. [from: Betty; item id 221]
  - Greenman's ale x1 - Buy in the Dragon Inn pub in Yanille or on the Grand Exchange (about 150 gp). [item id 1909]
  - Rotten tomato x1 - Buy in the shop in Canifis. [item id 2518]
  - Fruit blast x1 - Any except the ready-made one from the shop: mix it in the Tree Gnome Stronghold or buy on the Grand Exchange. [item id 2084]
  - Ashes x1 - Stays after any fire. [item id 592]
- In the game (RuneLite plugin):
  - NPCs highlighted: Cook
  - Items highlighted in the bag: Eye of newt, Greenman's ale, Rotten tomato, Fruit blast, Ashes
  - Dialogue choices to pick: “Do you have any other quests for me?” → “Angry! It makes me angry!” → “What seems to be the problem?” → “YES”
  - Marked done automatically when: quest completed: Recipe for Disaster - Another Cook's Quest
- Walkthrough:
  1. Talk to the Cook in Lumbridge Castle's kitchen. Dialogue: “Do you have any other quests for me?” → “Angry! It makes me angry!” (or “I don't really care to be honest.”) → “What seems to be the problem?” → “YES”.
  2. Use the Ashes on the Fruit blast to make a Dirty blast.
  3. Talk to the Cook again with the Eye of newt, Greenman's ale, Rotten tomato and Dirty blast. Dialogue: “About those ingredients you wanted for the banquet...”.
  4. Enter the castle dining room through the door west of the kitchen and watch the cutscene.

## Part 3 - Target levels at the end of each stage

The levels the route itself gives you by the end of each stage. A single number is guaranteed by the quests and training steps of the route; a range is the guaranteed level up to what the likely extras reach (Strength 25 instead of 20, iron ore, the Daero option). Cells with a dashed border belong to the optional Bond branch (stage 6): they are targets of steps you may skip.

| Skill | Stage 1 | Stage 2 | Stage 3 | Stage 4 | Stage 5 | Stage 6 | Stage 7 | Stage 8 | Stage 9 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Attack | 1 | 20 | 30 | 40 | 40 | 40 | 49 | 49 | 49–52 |
| Strength | 1 | 1 | 20–25 | 20–25 | 35–36 | 35–36 | 39–40 | 39–40 | 39–47 |
| Defence | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 33 | 33 | 33 | 34 | 34–43 |
| Ranged | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 50 | 1 | 30 | 30 |
| Magic | 1 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 55 | 27 | 27 | 28 |
| Prayer | 11 | 11 | 11 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 43 | 43 |
| Woodcutting | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 60 | 15 | 36 | 36 |
| Firemaking | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 | 15 |
| Fishing | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 50 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| Cooking | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 50 | 4 | 10 | 10 |
| Mining | 10 | 10 | 15–17 | 15–17 | 15–17 | 60 | 15–17 | 15–17 | 15–17 |
| Smithing | 1 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 |
| Crafting | 7 | 8 | 8 | 8 | 8 | 8 | 8 | 31 | 31 |
| Runecraft | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| Agility | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 31 | 31–40 | 31 |
| Slayer | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 20 | 20 |
| Quest points | 6 | 32 | 40–41 | 43–44 | 45–46 | 45–46 | 55–56 | 59–61 | 68–70 |

These are the levels the route produces, not a training plan: Defence stays at 1 until Dragon Slayer I, and only the training steps name a target (Attack 30/40, Strength 20, Magic 25, Mining 10, Woodcutting 36, Ranged 30, Prayer 43...). Stage 5 gives Defence 33 and Strength 35 on its own: Dragon Slayer I pays 18,650 XP in each. Quest points count the optional Shield of Arrav (S3-05) and, from stage 8, the optional A Porcine of Interest (a task of S8-04) in the top of the range. Attack 60 for the Dragon scimitar is not guaranteed by this route: Monkey Madness I pays 35,000 XP in the pair you choose (Daero's training), which is Attack 52 at most from here. The values were recomputed from the route rewards on 13 Oct 2026.
